----------------------------------------------------------------
Form Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : DUNDAR_2014 / Uygulama : PFRM7S
Makro Kodu : BOMU01-02
Hangi Tablo için : BOMU21
Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için : BOMU01
Açýklama : ÜRÜN AÐACI EXCELE-YAN ÇÝZGÝ SIRALI_RESÝMLÝ_SAYFA AYARLI_SADE-8 PUNTA
Kod : 
Public k(20),satir,sheet,ExcelApp,wb1,mamulkodu,SatirSayisi,SonSatir,SonSutun

Sub PrintForm()

	Dim j, S0P1, S0P2, S0P3,ACIKLAMA_KOLON_BASLANGICI, seviye

	Set ExcelApp = CreateObject("Excel.Application")
	Set Wb1 = ExcelApp.Workbooks.Add
	Set Sheet = Wb1.ActiveSheet

	ExcelApp.Visible = False
	baslangic_satiri = 2
	maxseviye = 0
	SatirSayisi = MULTILEVEL.GetRecCount()

	Grid1=Doc.GetGridColInfo("","MULTILEVEL")
	KolonSayisi1 = Ubound(Grid1)
	
	For i = 1 To SatirSayisi
		Grid1 = Doc.GetGridColInfo("","MULTILEVEL")
		KolonSayisi1 = Ubound(Grid1)
		For KolonNo1 = 0 To KolonSayisi1
			FieldName1 = Grid1(KolonNo1, 0)
			If FieldName1 = "PICTURE_LEVEL1" Then
				SatirDegeri = Trim(MULTILEVEL.GetFieldValue(FieldName1, i))
				If Right(SatirDegeri, 1) > maxseviye Then
					maxseviye = Right(SatirDegeri, 1)
				End If
			End If
		Next
	Next

	BASLANGIC_KOLONU = 2
	ACIKLAMA_KOLON_BASLANGICI = BASLANGIC_KOLONU+maxseviye+3	
	KOLON_BASLIK_KONUMU=0
	
	For Satir = 1 To SatirSayisi
		Grid = Doc.GetGridColInfo("","MULTILEVEL")
		KolonSayisi = Ubound(Grid)
		For KolonNo = 0 To KolonSayisi
			FieldName = Grid(KolonNo, 0)
			If FieldName = "PICTURE_LEVEL1" Then
				SatirDegeri = Trim(MULTILEVEL.GetFieldValue(FieldName, Satir))
				Seviye = Right(SatirDegeri,1) + 1
				
				If Satir=1 Then Wb1.Sheets(1).cells(baslangic_satiri,1).Value = "Resim"
				FieldName = "BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE" 
				KaynakKodu = Trim(MULTILEVEL.GetFieldValue(FieldName, Satir))
		
				Set S0P = Doc.Select1("dddtyy", "STOK0P", "EVRAKNO='" & KaynakKodu & "'", "SUBSRNUM", -1)
				If S0P.GetRowCount > 0 Then
					Resim1 = Trim(Doc.Getd7Directory_DataFirm & "docs\" & Trim(S0P.FULLFILENAME1(1)))
				Else
					'FieldName1 = "BOMREC_INPUTTYPE" 
					'SatirDegeri1 = Trim(MULTILEVEL.GetFieldValue(FieldName1, Satir))

					'If SatirDegeri1 = "H" Then
					'	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("STOK00", "")
						
					'	Doc1.Load_Voucher KaynakKodu
					'	Set S0P2 = Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK0P")	
					'	S0P2.FULLFILENAME1(1) = "RESIMCEK.jpg"
					'	Doc1.Save_Voucher
					'	Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher
					'ElseIf SatirDegeri1 = "I" Then
						Resim1 = ""
					'End If
				End If
				'Wb1.Sheets(1).cells(baslangic_satiri+Satir, 1).Value = Resim1

				If Resim1 <> "" Then
					Wb1.Sheets(1).cells(baslangic_satiri+Satir,1).rowheight = Wb1.Sheets(1).cells(baslangic_satiri+Satir,1).rowheight*2.5
					rtop = Wb1.Sheets(1).cells(baslangic_satiri+Satir,1).Top+1.5
					rleft= Wb1.Sheets(1).cells(baslangic_satiri+Satir,1).Left+1.5
					Wb1.Sheets(1).cells(baslangic_satiri+Satir, 1).Select
					Set resim = Wb1.ActiveSheet.Pictures.Insert(Trim(resim1)) 
					resimadi=resim.name
					wb1.ActiveSheet.Shapes(resimadi).Select
					resim.Left = rleft 
					resim.Top = rtop
					ExcelApp.selection.ShapeRange.LockAspectRatio = msoFalse
					ExcelApp.selection.ShapeRange.Height = 10
					ExcelApp.selection.ShapeRange.Width = 25
				End If
					
				FieldName = "BOMREC_SIRANO" 
				If Satir=2 Then Wb1.Sheets(1).cells(baslangic_satiri,2).Value = "Op.No"
				SiraNo = Trim(MULTILEVEL.GetFieldValue(FieldName, Satir))
				Wb1.Sheets(1).cells(baslangic_satiri+Satir, 2).Value = SiraNo
				
				FieldName = "BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE" 
				If Satir=1 Then Wb1.Sheets(1).cells(baslangic_satiri,3).Value = "Kodlar"
				KaynakKodu = Trim(MULTILEVEL.GetFieldValue(FieldName, Satir))
				Wb1.Sheets(1).cells(baslangic_satiri+Satir, BASLANGIC_KOLONU+seviye).Value = KaynakKodu
				
				KOLON_BASLIK_KONUMU = ACIKLAMA_KOLON_BASLANGICI 
				
				FieldName = "ACIKLAMA" 
				If Satir=1 Then Wb1.Sheets(1).cells(baslangic_satiri,ACIKLAMA_KOLON_BASLANGICI).Value = FieldName
				Aciklama = Trim(MULTILEVEL.GetFieldValue(FieldName, Satir))
				Wb1.Sheets(1).cells(baslangic_satiri+Satir, KOLON_BASLIK_KONUMU).Value = Aciklama
				
				KOLON_BASLIK_KONUMU = ACIKLAMA_KOLON_BASLANGICI + 1
				
				FieldName = "BOMREC_OPERASYON_IMLT01_AD" 
				If Satir=1 Then Wb1.Sheets(1).cells(baslangic_satiri, KOLON_BASLIK_KONUMU).Value = "OPERASYON"
				Operasyon = Trim(MULTILEVEL.GetFieldValue(FieldName, Satir))
				Wb1.Sheets(1).cells(baslangic_satiri+Satir, KOLON_BASLIK_KONUMU).Value = Operasyon
				
				KOLON_BASLIK_KONUMU = ACIKLAMA_KOLON_BASLANGICI + 2
				
				FieldName = "BOMREC_KAYNAK0" 
				If Satir=1 Then Wb1.Sheets(1).cells(baslangic_satiri, KOLON_BASLIK_KONUMU).Value = "Hammadde Miktarý / Tezgah Süresi"
				Miktar_Sure = Trim(MULTILEVEL.GetFieldValue(FieldName, Satir))
				Wb1.Sheets(1).cells(baslangic_satiri+Satir, KOLON_BASLIK_KONUMU).Value = Miktar_Sure
				
				KOLON_BASLIK_KONUMU = ACIKLAMA_KOLON_BASLANGICI + 3
				
				FieldName = "KAYNAK_BIRIM" 
				If Satir=1 Then Wb1.Sheets(1).cells(baslangic_satiri, KOLON_BASLIK_KONUMU).Value = "Hammadde Miktarý / Tezgah Süresi"
				Birim = Trim(MULTILEVEL.GetFieldValue(FieldName, Satir))
				Wb1.Sheets(1).cells(baslangic_satiri+Satir, KOLON_BASLIK_KONUMU).Value = Birim
				
				KOLON_BASLIK_KONUMU = ACIKLAMA_KOLON_BASLANGICI + 4
				
				FieldName = "BOMREC_YMAMULPM" 
				If Satir=1 Then Wb1.Sheets(1).cells(baslangic_satiri, KOLON_BASLIK_KONUMU).Value = "Yarý Mamül Miktarý"
				YariMamulMiktari = Trim(MULTILEVEL.GetFieldValue(FieldName, Satir))
				Wb1.Sheets(1).cells(baslangic_satiri+Satir, KOLON_BASLIK_KONUMU).Value = YariMamulMiktari
				
			End If
		Next
	Next

	SonSatir = Sheet.Cells(1, "A").SpecialCells(11).Row
	SonSutun = Sheet.Cells.SpecialCells(11).Column   'xlCellTypeLastCell kodunun karþýlýðý 11 ' dir.

	Wb1.ActiveSheet.Cells.Select
	With ExcelApp.Selection.Font
		.Name = "Agency FB"
		.Size = 8
	End With

	Sheet.Range(Sheet.Cells(baslangic_satiri, ACIKLAMA_KOLON_BASLANGICI+2), Sheet.Cells(SonSatir, ACIKLAMA_KOLON_BASLANGICI)).Select
	ExcelApp.Selection.NumberFormat = "#,##0.00"

	Sheet.Range(Sheet.Cells(baslangic_satiri, ACIKLAMA_KOLON_BASLANGICI+4), Sheet.Cells(SonSatir, ACIKLAMA_KOLON_BASLANGICI)).Select
	ExcelApp.Selection.NumberFormat = "#,##0.00"

	Wb1.Sheets(1).cells(baslangic_satiri-1,SonSutun).Select
	ExcelApp.Selection.WrapText = True

	Sheet.Cells.Select
	ExcelApp.Selection.RowHeight = 12
	ExcelApp.Selection.VerticalAlignment = 2 'xlTop

	Wb1.Sheets(1).Rows(1).RowHeight = 20
	Wb1.Sheets(1).Rows(baslangic_satiri).RowHeight = 25
		
	'BAÞLIK BOYA
	For i = 1 to SonSutun
		Sheet.Cells(baslangic_satiri, i).Select
		'ExcelApp.Selection.Font.Bold = True
		ExcelApp.Selection.Font.Size = 8
		ExcelApp.Selection.Interior.ColorIndex = 19   '15:GRI,1:SIYAH,2:beyaz,3:kýrmýzý,4:yeþil,5:mavi,6:sarý,7:pembe,8:magenta,9:koyukahve,10:KOYUYEÞÝL,11:LACIVERT,12:BOKYESILI,12:MOR
		ExcelApp.Selection.Interior.Pattern =1 '1:xlSolid,2:noktalý3:çizgili
		ExcelApp.Selection.Font.ColorIndex = 1 '1:SÝYAH 2:GRÝ,3:KIRMIZI
		ExcelApp.Selection.WrapText = True 
		ExcelApp.Selection.HorizontalAlignment = 3 'xlCenter 
		ExcelApp.Selection.VerticalAlignment = 2 'xlCenter 
	Next
	
	If Resim2 <> "" THEN
		rtop = Wb1.Sheets(1).cells(1,1).Top+1.5
		rleft= Wb1.Sheets(1).cells(1,1).Left+1.5
		Wb1.Sheets(1).cells(1, SonSutun).Select
		Set resim = Wb1.ActiveSheet.Pictures.Insert(trim(resim2)) 
		resimadi=resim.name
		wb1.ActiveSheet.Shapes(resimadi).Select
		resim.Left = rleft 
		resim.Top = rtop
		ExcelApp.selection.ShapeRange.LockAspectRatio = msoFalse
		ExcelApp.selection.ShapeRange.Height = 15
		ExcelApp.selection.ShapeRange.Width = 30
	End If
	Resim2 = ""

	'RESÝM KOLONU AYARI
	Wb1.Sheets(1).Columns(1).ColumnWidth = 5
	Wb1.ActiveSheet.Columns(1).Select
	ExcelApp.Selection.HorizontalAlignment= 3 'xlCenter

	For g = 3 to ACIKLAMA_KOLON_BASLANGICI
		Wb1.ActiveSheet.Columns(g).Select
		ExcelApp.Selection.HorizontalAlignment= 1
		if g = 3 Then
			ExcelApp.Selection.ColumnWidth = 2
		elseif g = ACIKLAMA_KOLON_BASLANGICI Then
			ExcelApp.Selection.ColumnWidth = 7.5
		Else
			ExcelApp.Selection.ColumnWidth = 3
		End If
	Next
	
	For h = ACIKLAMA_KOLON_BASLANGICI-1 to ACIKLAMA_KOLON_BASLANGICI+2
		Wb1.ActiveSheet.Columns(h).Select
		ExcelApp.Selection.HorizontalAlignment= 1
		Wb1.ActiveSheet.Columns(h).autofit
	Next
	
	For h = ACIKLAMA_KOLON_BASLANGICI+2 to SonSutun
		Wb1.ActiveSheet.Columns(h).Select
		ExcelApp.Selection.HorizontalAlignment= 1
		ExcelApp.Selection.ColumnWidth = 9
	Next

	For h = 2 to 2
		Wb1.Sheets(1).Columns(h).ColumnWidth = 3
		Wb1.ActiveSheet.Columns(h).Select
		ExcelApp.Selection.HorizontalAlignment= 3 'xlCenter
	Next

	Sheet.Range(Sheet.Cells(baslangic_satiri-1, 1), Sheet.Cells(baslangic_satiri-1, SonSutun)).Merge
	Wb1.Sheets(1).cells(1,1).value = Wb1.Sheets(1).cells(baslangic_satiri+1,3).value & " - " & Wb1.Sheets(1).cells(baslangic_satiri+1,ACIKLAMA_KOLON_BASLANGICI).value
	Wb1.Sheets(1).cells(1,1).Select
	ExcelApp.Selection.Font.Bold = True
	ExcelApp.Selection.Font.Size = 10
	'Sheet.Range(Sheet.Cells(baslangic_satiri-2, 3), Sheet.Cells(baslangic_satiri, ACIKLAMA_KOLON_BASLANGICI)).Merge
	Wb1.Sheets(1).cells(1,1).Select
	Wb1.ActiveSheet.Rows(2).Select
	ExcelApp.Selection.HorizontalAlignment= 3 'xlCenter
	Wb1.Sheets(1).cells.Select

	ExcelApp.Selection.Borders(1).LineStyle = 1  		'.Borders(1):sol dik çizgi,.Borders(3)=üst yatay çizgi,(4):alt yatay çizgi, (5):çapraz \,(6):çapraz:/,(10):sað dik çizgi
	ExcelApp.Selection.Borders(1).Weight = 1  			'1:noktalý,2:çizgi
	ExcelApp.Selection.Borders(4).LineStyle = 1			'.Borders(4):alt yatay çigi
	ExcelApp.Selection.Borders(4).Weight = 1  			'1:noktalý,2:çizgi
	ExcelApp.Selection.Borders(3).LineStyle = 1			'.Borders(3):üst yatay çigi
	ExcelApp.Selection.Borders(3).Weight = 1			'1:noktalý,2:çizgi
	ExcelApp.Selection.Borders(10).LineStyle = 1  	'.Border(10):seçili alanýn en sað dik çizgisi
	ExcelApp.Selection.Borders(10).Weight = 1  			'1:noktalý,2:çizgi, 4:kalýn
	Call Yaziciya_Yazdir
	ExcelApp.Visible = True
	Set Wb1 = Nothing
	Set ExcelApp = Nothing

End Sub
'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Sub Yaziciya_Yazdir ()
 	Sheet.Range(Sheet.Cells(1, 1),Sheet.Cells(SonSatir,SonSutun)).Select
	ExcelApp.Selection.Name = "YAZILACAK_ALAN" 
	Sheet.PageSetup.PrintArea = 	"YAZILACAK_ALAN"

	With Sheet.PageSetup
  		'.CenterFooter = "Page &P of &N"
		.LeftMargin = ExcelApp.InchesToPoints(0.39)
     .RightMargin = ExcelApp.InchesToPoints(0)
     .TopMargin = ExcelApp.InchesToPoints(0.19)
     .BottomMargin = ExcelApp.InchesToPoints(0.19)
     .HeaderMargin = ExcelApp.InchesToPoints(0.02)
     .FooterMargin = ExcelApp.InchesToPoints(0.02)
     .Zoom = 100
     .PrintErrors = xlPrintErrorsDisplayed
		'.PaperSize = 1
     .OddAndEvenPagesHeaderFooter = False
     .DifferentFirstPageHeaderFooter = False
     '.ScaleWithDocHeaderFooter = True
     '.AlignMarginsHeaderFooter = True
		.CenterHorizontally = False
		.CenterVertically = False
		.Orientation = 2
		.Draft = False
		'.PrintTitleRows = "$1:$2"
		.PrintTitleColumns = ""
	End With
End Sub

Kod : 
Public Excel, Wb, Sh1, Grid, S88, siraNo, satir, CHKodu, diptoplam_agr, diptoplamnet, diptoplamkasa, ToplamBrutAgirlik
Public baslangic,packing,siparisno,tam_paletyukseligi,palet_ebat,STK88T,STK88E, PaletNo, EskiPaletNo

Sub PrintForm()
	
	Set STK88E = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK88E")
	Set STK88T = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK88T")
	
	EskiPaletNo = ""
	STOK88T.Safepack()
	Set Excel = CreateObject("Excel.Application")
	Set Wb = Excel.WorkBooks.Add
	Set Sh1 = Wb.Sheets(1)
	
	baslangic = 30
	satirNo1 = 0
	satirNo1 = satirNo1 + 1
	ToplamBrutAgirlik = 0
	diptoplamnet = 0
	diptoplamkasa = 0
	siparisno = ""
	tam_paletyukseligi = 0
	palet_ebat = ""
	
	dosya = "PACKING"
	DosyaAdi = Doc.GetD7Directory_DataFirm & "ABDAT\" & Replace(Doc.BuGun, "/", "") & "-" &  Doc.Saat(4) & "-" & LEFT(trim(dosya),7) & ".xls"
	' DosyaAdi = Doc.GetD7Directory_DataFirm & "ABDAT\" & Replace(Doc.BuGun, "/", "") & "-" & LEFT(trim(dosya),7) & ".xls"
	
	Set S88 = Doc.Select2("A0B0C","STOK88T","","", -1)
	
	Call Alanlar()
	
	siraNo = 0
	satir = 0
	Call Print_form_sayfabasi()
	
	For k = 1 To S88.GetRowCount()
		Call Print_form_satir(k)
	Next
	
	Call DipToplam()
	
	Wb.SaveAs(DosyaAdi)
	Excel.Visible = True
	
End Sub
Sub Print_form_sayfabasi()

	Wb.Sheets(1).cells(1, 1).Select
	Set resim = Wb.ActiveSheet.Pictures.Insert(trim(logo))
	resimadi=resim.name
	wb.ActiveSheet.Shapes(resimadi).Select
	resim.Left = 1
	resim.Top = 1
	Excel.selection.ShapeRange.LockAspectRatio = msoFalse
	Excel.selection.ShapeRange.Height = 60
	Excel.selection.ShapeRange.Width = 120

	Sh1.Rows("22:100").Select
	Excel.Selection.RowHeight = 15

	Sh1.Cells(1, 1).Select

	Excel.Selection.Font.Bold = True
	Excel.Selection.Font.Size = 25

	Baslik = "Faks / Fax :"

	Sh1.Cells(8, 4).Select
	Excel.Selection.Font.Bold = False
	Excel.ActiveCell.Characters(1, Len(Baslik)).Font.Bold = True

	Sh1.Cells(9, 1).Value = "E-Posta / Mail:"
	Sh1.Cells(10, 1).Value = "Fatura No / Invoice No:"
	Sh1.Cells(11, 1).Value = "Ýrsaliye No / Despatch No:"
	Sh1.Cells(12, 1).Value = "Teslim Þekli / Delivery Terms:"
	Sh1.Cells(13, 1).Value = "Ödeme Þekli / Payment Terms:"
	Sh1.Cells(14, 1).Value = "Referans:"
	Sh1.Cells(15, 1).Value = "Nakliye Þekli / Transport Type:"
	Sh1.Cells(16, 1).Value = "Nakliye Firmasý / Transport Company:"
	Sh1.Cells(17, 1).Value = "Adres / Adress:"
	Sh1.Cells(18, 1).Value = "Telefon / Phone:"
	Sh1.Cells(19, 1).Value = "Ýlgili Kiþi / Responsible:"
	
	despatch = STOK88E.NOTES_4
	packing = STOK88E.EVRAKNO
	nakliyeci = STOK88E.BOX1_1
	nakliyeci1 = STOK88E.NAKLIYECIFIRMA
	nakliyeci2 = STOK88E.NAKLIYEKOSULLARI
	nakliyeci = trim(nakliyeci)

	Delivery = Doc.GetUDFValue("STOK88E",packing,"DELIVERY",0)
	Payment = Doc.GetUDFValue("STOK88E",packing,"PAYMENT",0)
	Shipment = Doc.GetUDFValue("STOK88E",packing,"SHIPMENT",0)
	Nakliye = Doc.GetUDFValue("STOK88E",packing,"NAKLIYE",0)
	
'	Sh1.Cells(9, 3).Value = "+90 216 593 19 55"

	Sh1.Cells(9, 4).Value = "ugozeten@sa-ba.com.tr"
	Sh1.Cells(10, 4).Value = ""

	Sh1.Cells(12, 4).Value = trim(Delivery)
	Sh1.Cells(13, 4).Value = trim(Payment)
	Sh1.Cells(14, 4).Value = trim(Shipment)
	Sh1.Cells(15, 4).Value = trim(Nakliye)
	Sh1.Cells(16, 4).Value = trim(nakliyeci)
	Sh1.Cells(17, 4).Value = trim(nakliyeci1)
	Sh1.Cells(18, 4).Value = trim(nakliyeci2)

	Sh1.Cells(7, 9).Value = chadres1

	Sh1.Cells(9, 9).Value = chadres3

	tel = doc.GetDBFieldValue("CARI00","TELEFONNO_1",CHKodu,"")
	fax = doc.GetDBFieldValue("CARI00","FAXNO",CHKodu,"")
	email = doc.GetDBFieldValue("CARI00","EMAIL",CHKodu,"")
	
	Sh1.Cells(11, 6).Value = "Telefon / Phone :"

	Sh1.Cells(13, 6).Value = "E-Posta / Mail :"

	Sh1.Cells(11, 9).Value = Tel
	Sh1.Cells(12, 9).Value = Fax
	Sh1.Cells(13, 9).Value = eMail
	
	Sh1.Cells(15, 6).Value = "Müþteri / Ship To:"
	Sh1.Cells(15, 9).Value = sevkadres
	Sh1.Cells(16, 9).Value = sevkadres1
	Sh1.Cells(17, 9).Value = sevkadres2
	Sh1.Cells(18, 9).Value = sevkadres3
	Sh1.Cells(19, 9).Value = "-"
	
	Call SBaslik(i)
	
End Sub

Sub SBaslik(satirNo)
	Redim xCizgi(0)
	siraNo = siraNo + 1
	
	For i = 0 To UBound(Grid,1)
		Baslik = Grid(i,2)
		Sh1.Cells(21, i+1).Value = Baslik
		
		Sh1.Cells(21, i+1).Select
		Excel.Selection.HorizontalAlignment = 3
		Excel.Selection.VerticalAlignment = 4
		
		Excel.Selection.Font.Bold = True
		Excel.Selection.Font.Italic = True
		Excel.Selection.Font.Underline = 2
	Next
End Sub

Sub Print_form_satir(satirNo)
	siraNo = siraNo + 1
	satir = 20 + siraNo
	
	PaletNo = S88.PACKNO_1(satirNo)
	if PaletNo <> EskiPaletNo Then
		Call GetPaletOlculeri(PaletNo, PaletEbat, NetAgirlik, BrutAgirlik, PaletSatirSayisi)
		diptoplamnet = diptoplamnet + NetAgirlik
		ToplamBrutAgirlik = ToplamBrutAgirlik + BrutAgirlik
		YeniPalet = True

		Sh1.Range(Sh1.Cells(satir,10), Sh1.Cells(satir + PaletSatirSayisi - 1,10)).Select
		Excel.Selection.Merge
		Excel.Selection.VerticalAlignment = 2

		Sh1.Range(Sh1.Cells(satir,11), Sh1.Cells(satir + PaletSatirSayisi - 1,11)).Select
		Excel.Selection.Merge
		Excel.Selection.VerticalAlignment = 2

		Sh1.Range(Sh1.Cells(satir,12), Sh1.Cells(satir + PaletSatirSayisi - 1,12)).Select
		Excel.Selection.Merge
		Excel.Selection.VerticalAlignment = 2
	Else
		YeniPalet = False
		PaletEbat = ""
		BrutAgirlik = 0
		NetAgirlik = 0
	End if
	EskiPaletNo = PaletNo

	AlanDegeri = ""

	For i=0 To UBound(Grid,1)
		SutunGenisligi = Grid(i,1)
		AlanAdi = Grid(i,0)
		
		StokKod = S88.KOD(satirNo)
		
		if Left(AlanAdi, 1) <> "_" Then
			AlanDegeri = S88.GetFieldValue(AlanAdi,satirNo)
		Elseif Alanadi = "_AD" then
			AlanDegeri = doc.GetDbFieldValue("STOK00","AD",StokKod,"")			
		Elseif Alanadi = "_NETAGIRLIK" then
			AlanDegeri = NetAgirlik
		Elseif Alanadi = "_BRUTAGIRLIK" then
			AlanDegeri = BrutAgirlik
		Elseif Alanadi = "_EBAT" then
			AlanDegeri = PaletEbat
		Elseif Alanadi = "_BOX" then
			ret = GetAmbalajBilgileri(S88.KOD(satirNo), STOK88E.MUSTERIKODU, STOK88E.SHIPTOCODE, kasaiciadet, kasaagirligi, kasayuksekligi, palettebirsiradakiadet)
			KasaSayisi = Doc.Ceil(Doc.Div(S88.SF_MIKTAR(satirNo), kasaiciadet))
			AlanDegeri = kasasayisi
			diptoplamkasa = diptoplamkasa + kasasayisi
		Elseif Alanadi = "_NO" then
			AlanDegeri = satirNo
			
		Elseif Alanadi = "_ORDERSIP" then
			SipNo = S88.SIPNO(satirNo)
			AlanDegeri = doc.GetDbFieldValue("STOK40E","CHSIPNO",SipNo,"")
			
		Elseif Alanadi = "_ITEM" then
			AlanDegeri = doc.GetCHStokAdi(StokKod,CHKodu)
			
		Elseif Alanadi = "_MSKOD" then
			AlanDegeri = doc.GetCHStokKodu(StokKod,CHKodu)
		End if
			
		srnum = S88.SRNUM(satirNo)
		srnum = Doc.Cdbl(srnum)
		
		STK88T.SF_VRI_NUM1(srnum) = Doc.Ceil(tam_paletsayisi)
		
		If Grid(i,4) = "N" Then
			Ondalik = Grid(i,6)
			AlanDegeri = Doc.NumFormat(AlanDegeri, 18,Ondalik)
		ElseIf Grid(i,4) = "D" Then
			AlanDegeri = Doc.StrtoNordate(AlanDegeri)
		End If
		
		Sh1.Cells(satir, i+1).Value = AlanDegeri
		'  Sütun geniþliklerini ayarlma
		Sh1.Cells(satir, i+1).Select
		Excel.Selection.ColumnWidth = SutunGenisligi	
	Next
End Sub

Sub DipToplam()
	Set S88T2 = Doc.Select2("wef43wfdfgd", "STOK88T", "", "", -1)
	sayi = S88T2.GetRowCount()
	S88T2.GroupBy("EVRAKNO")
	
	Set S88TP = Doc.Select2G("wef43wfdffffffrgd", "STOK88T", "", "", "PACKNO_1", -1)
	PaletSayisi = S88TP.GetRowCount()

	satir = satir + 1
	
'		diptoplamnet = diptoplamnet + totalnetagr
'		ToplamBrutAgirlik = ToplamBrutAgirlik + BrutAgirlik

	For i=0 To UBound(Grid,1)
		SutunGenisligi = Grid(i,1)
		AlanAdi = Grid(i,0)
		
		if AlanAdi = "_BOX" Then
			AlanDegeri = diptoplamkasa
		Elseif AlanAdi = "SF_SF_UNIT" Then
			AlanDegeri = "Total :"
		Elseif AlanAdi = "_NETAGIRLIK" Then
			AlanDegeri = diptoplamnet
		Elseif AlanAdi = "NETAGIRLIK" Then
			AlanDegeri = ""
		Elseif AlanAdi = "_BRUTAGIRLIK" Then
			AlanDegeri = ToplamBrutAgirlik
		Elseif AlanAdi = "_PALLET" Then
			AlanDegeri = PaletSayisi
		Else
			AlanDegeri = S88T2.GetFieldValue(AlanAdi,1)
		End if
		
		If Grid(i,4) = "N" Then
			Ondalik = Grid(i,6)
			AlanDegeri = Doc.NumFormat(AlanDegeri, 18,Ondalik)
		ElseIf Grid(i,4) = "D" Then
			AlanDegeri = Doc.StrtoNordate(AlanDegeri)
		End If
		
		Sh1.Cells(satir, i+1).Value = AlanDegeri
		
		Sh1.Range(Sh1.Cells(satir, 6), Sh1.Cells(satir, 13)).Select
		Excel.Selection.Font.Bold = True
		Excel.Selection.Font.Italic = True
		
		
		if AlanAdi = "SF_SF_UNIT" Then
			Sh1.Cells(satir, i+1).Select
			Excel.Selection.Font.Bold = True
			'   Excel.Selection.Font.Size = 14
		End if
	Next
	
	STK88E.NOTES_2 = Doc.Ceil(PaletSayisi) & " Adet Palet"

	banka = Doc.GetUDFValue("STOK88E",packing,"BANKA",0)
	hesapno = Doc.GetUDFValue("STOK88E",packing,"HESAPNO",0)
	KTANot = Doc.GetUDFValue("STOK88E",packing,"NOT",0)
	
	Sh1.Cells(28, 2).Select
	Excel.Selection.Font.Bold = True
	Excel.Selection.Font.Italic = True
	
	Baslik = "Banka Bilgileri/ Bank Info : "
	Sh1.Cells(satir + 2, 1).Value = Baslik & trim(banka) & " " & trim(hesapno)
	Sh1.Cells(satir + 2, 1).Select
	Excel.Selection.Font.Bold = False
	Excel.ActiveCell.Characters(1, Len(Baslik)).Font.Bold = True

	Sh1.Cells(satir + 4, 1).Value = "Not1 : "
	Sh1.Cells(satir + 5, 1).Value = "Not2 : "
	Sh1.Cells(satir + 6, 1).Value = "Not3 : "
	Sh1.Cells(satir + 7, 1).Value = "Not4 : "
	
	Sh1.Cells(satir + 4, 2).Value = trim(STOK88E.NOTES_1)
	Sh1.Cells(satir + 5, 2).Value = trim(STOK88E.NOTES_2)
	Sh1.Cells(satir + 6, 2).Value = trim(STOK88E.NOTES_3)
	Sh1.Cells(satir + 7, 2).Value = trim(KTANot)
	
	Sh1.Cells(satir + 4, 5).Value = "Customs Broker / Gümrük Müþavirliði"
	Sh1.Cells(satir + 4, 5).Select
	Excel.Selection.Font.Bold = True
	
	Sh1.Cells(satir + 5, 5).Value = "Unsped Gümrük Müþavirliði A.Þ."
	Sh1.Cells(satir + 6, 5).Value = "Ýlgili : Atilla Güney , Özkan Yýlmaz"
	Sh1.Cells(satir + 7, 5).Value = "Tel : 0212 413 35 35 /3514 Fax : 0212 679 16 62"
	
	Sh1.Range(Sh1.Cells(21, 1), Sh1.Cells(21, 6)).Select
	Excel.Selection.VerticalAlignment = 2
	
	Sh1.Cells(1, 1).Select
	
End Sub


Sub Alanlar()

	Grid(0,0) = "PACKNO_1"
	Grid(1,0) = "_NO"

	Grid(3,0) = "_MSKOD"

	Grid(6,0) = "SF_MIKTAR"

	Grid(8,0) = "_BOX"
	Grid(9,0) = "_NETAGIRLIK"
	Grid(10,0) = "_BRUTAGIRLIK"
	Grid(11,0) = "_EBAT"
	
	Grid(0,1) = 5.86
	Grid(1,1) = 5.86
	Grid(2,1) = 17
	Grid(3,1) = 19.14
	Grid(4,1) = 42.71
	Grid(5,1) = 6.14
	Grid(6,1) = 8.57
	Grid(7,1) = 11.29
	Grid(8,1) = 9.14
	Grid(9,1) = 9.24
	Grid(10,1) = 9.24	
	Grid(11,1) = 14.57
	
	Grid(0,2) = "Pallet" & vblf & "No"
	Grid(1,2) = "Item" & vblf & "No"
	Grid(2,2) = "Kod / Code"
	Grid(3,2) = "Stok / Item Code"
	Grid(4,2) = "Ürün Tanýmý / Description"
	Grid(5,2) = "Birim /" & vblf & " Unit Type"
	Grid(6,2) = "Miktar / Quantity"
	Grid(7,2) = "Net Aðýrlýk / Net Weight"
	
	Grid(8,2) = "Koli Ad. / Num. of Box"
	Grid(9,2) = "Toplam Net / Total Net"
	Grid(10,2) = "Brüt /" & Chr(10) &" Gross Weight"

	Grid(0,4) = "C"

	Grid(6,4) = "N"
	Grid(7,4) = "N"

	Grid(9,4) = "N"

		If Grid(i,4)="N" Then
			AlanAdi = Grid(i,0)
			ondalik = STOK88T.MaxSigDig(AlanAdi)

		End If
	Next	
End Sub
Sub GetPaletOlculeri(PaletNo, PaletEbat, NetAgirlik, BrutAgirlik, PaletSatirSayisi)
	Set PLT = Doc.CreateTransactionalTableObject("ddwegwg=STACK", "IO")
	PLT.AddField2 "KASA_YUKSEKLIGI", "CHAR", 20, 0, "L", "", ""
	PLT.AddField2 "SIRA_SAYISI", "DOUBLE", 20, 6, "R", "", ""
	PLT.AddReportFields
	PLT.Empty

	PaletEbat = ""
	BrutAgirlik = 0

	Set S88T1 = Doc.Select2("sdg3h43hdf", "STOK88T", "PACKNO_1='" & PaletNo & "'", "SRNUM", -1)
	PaletSatirSayisi = S88T1.GetRowCount()
	Set A00 = Doc.Select1("asdegqwegsdg", "AMBLJ00", "KOD='" & S88T1.AMBALAJKODU1(1) & "'", "", -1)

	if A00.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
		PaletAgirligi = A00.AGIRLIK(1)
		PaletEni = A00.EN(1)
		PaletBoyu = A00.BOY(1)
		paletyuksekligi = A00.YUKSEKLIK(1)
	End if
	KapakAgirligi = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("AMBLJ00","AGIRLIK", S88T1.AMBALAJKODU2(1),"")

	chkod =  STOK88E.MUSTERIKODU
	Sevkadres = STOK88E.SHIPTOCODE
	For i = 1 to S88T1.GetRowCount()
		miktar = S88T1.SF_MIKTAR(i)
		stokkodu = S88T1.KOD(i)
		
		ret = GetAmbalajBilgileri(StokKodu, STOK88E.MUSTERIKODU, STOK88E.SHIPTOCODE, kasaiciadet, kasaagirligi, kasayuksekligi, palettebirsiradakiadet)
		if ret = 0 Then
			kasasayisi = Doc.Ceil(doc.div(miktar,kasaiciadet))

			kasatoplamagr = kasatoplamagr + kasaagirligi*kasasayisi
			totalnetagr = totalnetagr + S88T1.NETAGIRLIK(i)* S88T1.SF_MIKTAR(i)

			SiraSayisi = Doc.Div(kasasayisi, palettebirsiradakiadet)
			satirNo = PLT.AddRow		
			PLT.KASA_YUKSEKLIGI(satirNo) = kasayuksekligi
			PLT.SIRA_SAYISI(satirNo) = SiraSayisi
		End if
	Next
	if PLT.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
		PLT.GroupBy("KASA_YUKSEKLIGI")
		For i = 1 to PLT.GetRowCount()
			paletyuksekligi = paletyuksekligi + PLT.KASA_YUKSEKLIGI(i) * Doc.Ceil(PLT.SIRA_SAYISI(i))
		Next
	End if
	palet_ebat = PaletEni & "x" & PaletBoyu & "x" & paletyuksekligi
	PaletEbat = palet_ebat
	NetAgirlik = totalnetagr
	BrutAgirlik = 	NetAgirlik + kasatoplamagr + PaletAgirligi + KapakAgirligi
End Sub
Function GetAmbalajBilgileri(StokKodu, CHKodu, SevkAdresi, kasaiciadet, kasaagirligi, kasayuksekligi, palettebirsiradakiadet)
	kasaiciadet = 0
	kasaagirligi = 0
	palettebirsiradakiadet = 0

	GetAmbalajBilgileri = 0
	Set S0A = Doc.Select1("df123","STOK0A","EVRAKNO = '"& Stokkodu &"'&CH_KODU='" & CHKodu &"'&SHIPTOCODE='" & SevkAdresi & "'","",-1)
	if S0A.GetRowCount = 0 Then
		Set S0A = Doc.Select1("df123","STOK0A","EVRAKNO = '"& Stokkodu &"'&CH_KODU='" & CHKodu &"'","",-1)
		if S0A.GetRowCount() = 0 Then
			Set S0A = Doc.Select1("df123","STOK0A","EVRAKNO='" & Stokkodu & "&VARSAYILAN='E'","",-1)
		End if
	End if

	if S0A.GetRowCount = 0 Then 
		GetAmbalajBilgileri = -1
		Exit Function
	End if

	for j = 1 to S0A.GetRowCount
		if trim(S0A.AMBALAJTURU(j))="KASA" or trim(S0A.AMBALAJTURU(j))="KOLI" then
			kasaiciadet = S0A.SF_PAKETICERIGI(j)

			kasaagirligi = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00","B_AGIRLIK",trim(S0A.AMBALAJKODU(j)),"")

			kasayuksekligi = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00","B_YUKSELIK",trim(S0A.AMBALAJKODU(j)),"")

			palettebirsiradakiadet = Doc.Cdbl(S0A.NOTE_4(j))
			Exit For
		End if
	Next
	if (kasaiciadet * kasaagirligi * kasayuksekligi * palettebirsiradakiadet) = 0 Then GetAmbalajBilgileri = -1
End Function

		'End if

		If i = 1 Then
			res = pe1.line(Ust, 1, 0, sayfaBoyu)
		End If

		res = pe1.Nopen()

			OkResmi = Doc.Getd7Directory_DataFirm() & "IMAGES\okasagi.jpg"

			pay = 7

		res = pe1.SetBold(1)

		End If

		'End If

	'Altkonum = pe1.GetBottom

End Sub

Sub Etiket(pe1, i, Ust, Sol)
	'res = pe1.SetTransparentMode(0)
	res = pe1.SetBkgColor(RGB(255,0,0))
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 8)
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	Resim_yukariok = Doc.Getd7Directory_DataFirm() & "IMAGES\okyukari.jpg"

	LokStr = trim(STOK69T.LOCATION1(i)) & " " & trim(STOK69T.LOCATION2(i)) & " " & trim(STOK69T.LOCATION3(i))
	LokasyonBarkod = "--" & trim(STOK69T.LOCATION1(i)) & "-" & trim(STOK69T.LOCATION2(i)) & "-" & trim(STOK69T.LOCATION3(i))
	
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 90)
	res = pe1.Write(Ust, Sol+3 , 40, 135, LokStr)
	res = pe1.Picture(pe1.GetTop+7, pe1.GetRight+15, 23, 12, Resim_yukariok, 0)
		
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 24)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.NoPen()
	res = pe1.SetBarcodeParms(2,2)
	res = pe1.Barcode(pe1.GetTop,pe1.GetRight+15, 23, EtiketEni, "CODE39", LokasyonBarkod, "") 
End Sub                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  


----------------------------------------------------------------
Form Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : DUNDAR_2014 / Uygulama : PFRM7S
Makro Kodu : A_NORM
Hangi Tablo için : STOK20
Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için : D7KIDSLB MMPS10 STOK25 TRACK25
Açýklama : BARKODLU TANIMLAMA ETÝKETÝ
Kod : 
Public Ust, Sol, EtiketBoyu,EtiketEni, UstMargin, AltMargin, Solmargin, sagMargin, EtiketNo, d7LB
Sub PrintLabel()
	set pe1 = Doc.CreatePrintEngineOutput()

Doc.Save_Voucher()

	EtiketNo = 0
	sayfaBoyu = 150
	sayfaEni = 100
	Ustmargin = 7
	SolMargin = 5
	sagMargin = sayfaEni - 5
	EtiketBoyu = 68
	EtiketEni = 90
	EndenEtiketlerArasi = 1
	BoydanEtiketlerArasi = 3
	EndenEtiketSayisi = 1
	BoydanEtiketSayisi = 2

	EtiketFormu = SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME
	Set d7LB = Doc.RunMacro1("MAKR7S.ETIKET", "GetEtiketTable", EtiketFormu)

	res = pe1.Open(0,"Etiket",sayfaBoyu,sayfaEni,"")

	For i = 1 to D7LB.GetRowCount()
		EtiketMiktari = d7LB.SF_BAKIYE(i)
		CHKodu = d7LB.CH_MUSTERI(i)
		EtiketNo = EtiketNo + 1
		yEtiketSayisi = Doc.Floor((EtiketNo-1) / EndenEtiketSayisi) mod BoydanEtiketSayisi
		if EtiketNo Mod EndenEtiketSayisi = 1 or EndenEtiketSayisi = 1 then
			sol = SolMargin
		else
			sol = SolMargin + EtiketEni + EndenEtiketlerArasi
		end if
		if yEtiketSayisi = 0 and (EtiketNo Mod EndenEtiketSayisi = 1 or EndenEtiketSayisi = 1) and EtiketNo > 1 then
			res = pe1.PageBreak()
			Ust = Ustmargin					
		else
			Ust = Ustmargin + yEtiketSayisi * (EtiketBoyu + BoydanEtiketlerArasi)
		end if
		Call Etiket(pe1,i, EtiketMiktari, CHKodu)
	Next
	res = pe1.Show()
	Doc.ActivateAfterMacro(1)
End Sub
Sub Etiket(pe1,satirNo, EtiketMiktari, CHKodu)
	satirAra = 8
	Sag = sol + EtiketEni
	Alt = Ust + EtiketBoyu - UstMargin
' dikey çizgilerin yerleri
	D1 = Sol + 55

'yatay çizgilerin yerleri
	Y1 = Ust + 9
	Y2 = Ust + 11
	Y3 = Ust + 18
	Y31 = Ust + 24
	Y32 = Ust + 30
	Y4 = Ust + 41
	Y5 = Ust + 42.5
	Y6 = Ust + 52
	Y7 = Ust + 63

	res = pe1.PenSize(0.7)
	res = pe1.Box(Ust,Sol, Y1 - Ust, EtiketEni)
	res = pe1.Box(Y2,Sol, Y7 - Y2, EtiketEni)
	res = pe1.PenSize(0.4)
	res = pe1.Line(Y3,Sol, 0, EtiketEni)
	res = pe1.Line(Y31,Sol, 0, EtiketEni)
'	res = pe1.Line(Y32,Sol, 0, EtiketEni)
	res = pe1.Line(Y4,Sol, 0, EtiketEni)
'	res = pe1.Line(Y5,Sol, 0, EtiketEni)
	res = pe1.Line(Y6,Sol, 0, EtiketEni)
	res = pe1.Line(Y2,D1, Y31 - Y2, 0)

	logo = Doc.GetD7Directory_DataFirm() & Doc.Customp_gets("!LOGO")
	
	StokKodu = D7LB.SKOD(satirNo)
	StokAdi = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "AD", D7LB.SKOD(satirNo), "")
	LotNo = D7LB.LOTNUMBER(satirNo)
	SeriNo = D7LB.SERINO(satirNo)
	Tarih = Doc.StrToNorDate(D7LB.REFTARIH1(satirNo))
	Ondalik = Doc.SigDig(EtiketMiktari)
'	EtiketMiktari = Doc.NumFormat(EtiketMiktari,20,Ondalik)
''''''''''''''''''	EtiketMiktari = Doc.NumFormat(EtiketMiktari,20,Ondalik)
	CHKodu = CHKoduBul(StokKodu, satirNo)
	MStokKodu = Doc.GetCHStokKodu(StokKodu,CHKodu)
	MStokAdi = Doc.GetCHStokAdi(StokKodu,CHKodu)
	if Doc.GetDocType = "STOK20" Then
		Aciklama = doc.GetUDFValue("STOK20E",STOK20E.EVRAKNO,"ACIKLAMA",0)
	End if
	Genislik = 33
	res = pe1.NoPen()
	res = pe1.Picture(Ust + 0.5, sol + 1, Y1 - Ust - 1, 35, logo,114)

	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",10)
	res = pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(Ust + 3, sol + 40,15,EtiketEni - 40,"TANIMLAMA ETÝKETÝ")

	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.Write(Y2 + 1,sol + 1,5, D1 - sol - 2, "KOD/TANIM")
	res = pe1.Write(Y3 + 1, sol + 1,10, D1 - sol - 2, trim(MStokKodu))
	res = pe1.Write(Y31 + 1, sol + 1,5, sag - sol - 2,trim(MStokAdi))
	Barkod1 = trim(d7LB.KOD(satirNo))
	BarkodGen = Barkodgenisligi(Barkod1, 84)
	res = pe1.StoreSet(1)
	res = pe1.NoPen()
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 8)
	res = pe1.Barcode(pe1.GetTop + 4, sol + 4, 11, BarkodGen, "CODE128", BarKod1, "")
	res = pe1.UseSet(1)

'	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",8)
'	res = pe1.Write(Y2 + 1,D1 + 1,5, sag - D1 - 2, "PARTÝ/GKK RAPOR NO")
	res = pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",10)
	res = pe1.Write(Y2 + 1, D1, 5, sag - D1,"PARTÝ NO")
	res = pe1.Write(Y3 + 1, D1, 5, sag - D1,LotNo)

	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.Write(Y4 + 1, sol+1, 5, 17,"MÝKTAR")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight, 5, 30, ": " & EtiketMiktari & " " & D7LB.SF_UNIT(satirNo))

	Lokasyon = trim(d7LB.LOCATION1(satirNo)) & "-" & trim(d7LB.LOCATION2(satirNo)) & "-" & trim(d7LB.LOCATION3(satirNo)) & _
					"-" & trim(d7LB.LOCATION4(satirNo))
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + 5, sol+1, 5, 50,"LOKASYON : " & Lokasyon)

	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.Write(Y4 + 1, sol + 55, 5, sagMargin - sol - 55,"DÜNDARLAR REF. :")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + 5, pe1.GetLeft, 5, sagMargin - pe1.GetLeft, D7LB.SKOD(satirNo))

	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.Write(Y6 + 1, sol + 1, 5, EtiketEni, "AÇIKLAMA")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.getbottom, sol + 1, satiryuksekligi * 2, EtiketEni, Aciklama)

	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",8)
	res = pe1.Write(Y7 + 0.3, sol, 5, EtiketEni, "YAYIN TARIHI : 01.08.2003 DOKÜMAN NO : FR014/0")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",10)

End Sub
Function CHKoduBul(StokKodu, satirNo)
	Set T_STOK0GT = Doc.CreateRowSetObject("XXX=STOK0GT", "REPORT") 
	Set Kriterler = Doc.CreateSObject("Query")
	Kriterler.UseTable "STOK0GT"
	Kriterler.AddAndCriteria T_STOK0GT, "STOKCODE", "=", StokKodu 
	k = T_STOK0GT.RunQuery(Kriterler)
	if T_STOK0GT.GetRecCount = 0 then
		CHKoduBul = "" 
		exit Function
	elseif T_STOK0GT.GetRecCount = 1 then
		CHKoduBul = T_STOK0GT.KOD(1)
	elseif T_STOK0GT.GetRecCount > 1 then
		For i = 1 to T_STOK0GT.GetRecCount()
			Firma = Firma & i & ". " & trim(T_STOK0GT.KOD(i)) & " - " & trim(Doc.GetDBFieldValue("CARI00","AD",T_STOK0GT.KOD(i),"")) & vbcrlf 
		Next 
		k = inputbox(satirNo & ". satýr, Hangi firma için etiket basýlacak?" & vbcrlf & vbcrlf & Firma,"dinamo","1")
		if Doc.cDbl(k) > 0 and Doc.cDbl(k) <= T_STOK0GT.GetRecCount then
			CHKoduBul = T_STOK0GT.KOD(Doc.cDbl(k))
		else
			CHKoduBul = ""
		end if
	end if
End Function
Function Barkodgenisligi(Kod, MaxLen)
	Barkodgenisligi = (Len(trim(cstr(Kod))) + 1) * 4.6
	if Barkodgenisligi > MaxLen Then Barkodgenisligi = MaxLen
End Function

Public sayfaTuru, sayfaBoyu, sayfaEni, basla, bitir, sonSatir, satirSonu, ToplamEn , BasilabilirAlan
Public siraNo, satirBaslangic,UstMargin, AltMargin, sagMargin, solMargin, satirYuksekligi
Public AlanSayisi()	
Public xCizgi()
Public Grid
Sub PrintForm()
	ToplamEn = 0
	BasilabilirAlan = 0
	siraNo = 0

	sayfaBoyu = 210
	sayfaEni = 297
	UstMargin = 28
	AltMargin = sayfaBoyu - 10
	solMargin = 10
	sagMargin = sayfaEni - 10
	satiryuksekligi = 5
	SiraNo = 0
	satirBaslangic = ustmargin + satiryuksekligi*10

	Set pe1 = doc.CreatePrintEngineOutput()

	Call Alanlar()
	res = pe1.Open(0, "Satýnalma Talebi", SayfaEni ,SayfaBoyu,"LANDSCAPE")

	BasilabilirAlan = SayfaEni - 20
	satirSonu = sayfaBoyu - 50


	If doc.ErrorNumber Then
		MsgBox ("Hata main : " & doc.ErrorNumber)
		Exit Sub
	End If
	Dim satirSayisi_tableT
	satirSayisi_tableT = STOK47T.GetRecCount()
	Call Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
	For I = 1 To satirSayisi_tableT
		Call Print_form_satir(pe1, I)
	Next
		
	Call Print_form_sayfasonu(pe1)

	res = pe1.Show()
	res = Doc.ActivateAfterMacro(1)
End Sub

Sub Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
	ReDim xCizgi(0)

	res = pe1.StoreSet(1001)
	res = pe1.nopen()
	res = pe1.settransparentmode(0)
	res = pe1.box(8, 8, 30, SayfaEni-110)
	res = pe1.UseSet(1001)
	res = pe1.RemoveSet(1001)
        
	res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 10)
	res = pe1.nopen()
	logo = Doc.Getd7Directory_DataFirm() & trim(Doc.Customp_Gets("!LOGO"))
	res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 14)
	res = pe1.setbold(1)
	res = pe1.setalign("CENTER")
	res = pe1.write(UstMargin + 3, 10, 15, sagMargin - 10, "SATINALMA TEKLÝF ÝSTEME FORMU")
	res = pe1.setalign("LEFT")
	If logo <> "" Then
		res = pe1.Picture(12, 10, 15, 55, logo, 18)
	End If
	res = pe1.selectfont("Helvetica", "12")
	res = pe1.setbold(1)
	res = pe1.setalign("CENTER")
	res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", "10")

	res = pe1.setalign("LEFT")
'*******************
	BBasla = UstMargin + 15
	SatirAra = 6
	Genislik10 = 40
	Genislik11 = 120
	Genislik20 = 25
	Genislik21 = 80	


	FisNo = STOK47E.EVRAKNO(1)
	Tarih = doc.StrtoNordate(STOK47E.TARIH(1))
	TalepEdenBolum = STOK47E.CODEDBOLUM(1)
	TalepEdenBolumAdi = doc.GetGecoValue("DEPT","AD",TalepEdenBolum ,-1)
	TalepEdenKisi = STOK47E.CODEDKISI(1)  
	TalepEden = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("PERS00","AD",TalepEdenKisi,"")
	Tesisadi = STOK47E.GK_1_AD(1)
	'Ustyonetimoanayi = STOK47E.GK_2_AD(1)
	'Bolumyoneticisi = STOK47E.GK_3_AD(1)
	
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.write(BBasla , SolMargin+2, 6, Genislik10, "TALEP EDEN BÖLÜM")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.write(pe1.getTop , pe1.getRight + 1, 6, Genislik11, ": " & Trim(TalepEdenBolumAdi))
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.write(pe1.getTop , pe1.getRight + 1, 6, Genislik20, "TARÝH")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.write(pe1.getTop , pe1.getRight + 1, 6, Genislik21, ": " & Tarih)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.write(pe1.getTop + satirAra, SolMargin+2, 6, Genislik10, "TALEP EDEN KÝÞÝ")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.write(pe1.getTop , pe1.getRight + 1, 6, Genislik11, ": " & Trim(TalepEden))		
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.write(pe1.getTop , pe1.getRight + 1, 6, Genislik20, "TALEP NO")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.write(pe1.getTop , pe1.getRight + 1, 6, Genislik21, ": " & trim(FisNo))				
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)

res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	'res = pe1.write(BBasla , SolMargin+12, 20, Genislik10, "Tesis Adý")
	'res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	'res = pe1.write(pe1.getTop , pe1.getRight + 3, 20, Genislik11, ": " & Trim(Tesisadi))
	'res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	'res = pe1.write(pe1.getTop , pe1.getRight + 1, 6, Genislik20, "TARÝH")
	'res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	'res = pe1.write(pe1.getTop , pe1.getRight + 1, 6, Genislik21, ": " & Tarih)
	'res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	'res = pe1.write(pe1.getTop + satirAra, SolMargin+2, 6, Genislik10, "TALEP EDEN KÝÞÝ")
	'res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	'res = pe1.write(pe1.getTop , pe1.getRight + 1, 6, Genislik11, ": " & Trim(TalepEden))		
	'res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	'res = pe1.write(pe1.getTop , pe1.getRight + 1, 6, Genislik20, "TALEP NO")
	'res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	'res = pe1.write(pe1.getTop , pe1.getRight + 1, 6, Genislik21, ": " & trim(FisNo))				
	'res = pe1.SetBold(1)
'***********************		

	res = pe1.setalign("CENTER")
	res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 10)
	Sol = solmargin + 2
	For i = 0 To UBound(Grid,1)
		SutunGenisligi = Grid(i,1)
		Baslik = Grid(i,2)
		If SutunGenisligi > 0 Then	
			if i = 2 then
				SutunGenisligi = Grid(i,1) + Grid(i+1,1)
			end if
			res = pe1.write(ustmargin+satiryuksekligi*7, Sol, 8, SutunGenisligi - 4 , Baslik)
			SutunGenisligi = Grid(i,1)
			Sol = Sol + SutunGenisligi
			xCizgi(uBound(xCizgi)) =	Sol - 2
			redim Preserve xCizgi(uBound(xCizgi)+1)
		End If
	Next

	res = pe1.pensize(0.6)
'yatay çizgiler
	res = pe1.pensize(0.4)
	res = pe1.Line(28, 10, 0, SayfaEni-20)
	res = pe1.Line(satirBaslangic - 17, 10, 0, sayfaEni -20)
	res = pe1.Line(satirBaslangic - 4, 10, 0, sayfaEni -20)
	res = pe1.StoreSet(1001)
	res = pe1.PenColor(RGB(0, 0, 0))        
	res = pe1.pensize(0.5)

	Call Normal(pe1)
End Sub

Sub Print_form_satir(pe1, satirNo)
Dim satirpozr
	if trim(STOK47T.KOD(satirNo)) = "" then exit sub
	SiraNo = SiraNo + 1
	sonSatir = pe1.GetBottom()
	If sonSatir >= satirSonu Then
		Call Print_form_sayfasonu(pe1)
		res = pe1.PageBreak()
		Call Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
		satirpozr = satirBaslangic
	Else
		If satirNo = 1 Then
			satirpozr = satirBaslangic
		Else
			satirpozr = pe1.GetTop() + satiryuksekligi
		End If
	End If

	res = pe1.StoreSet(1001)
	res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 10)
	res = pe1.setbold(0)
	Sol = solMargin + 2
	res = pe1.setalign("LEFT")
	For i=0 To UBound(Grid,1)
		SutunGenisligi = Grid(i,1)
		DegiskenAdi = Grid(i,0)
		res = pe1.SetBold(0)
		if DegiskenAdi = "_CINS" then
			StokKodu = STOK47T.KOD(satirNo)
			Stokadi = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00","AD",StokKodu,"")
			if trim(STOK47T.STOK_NONSTOK(satirNo)) = "NS" Then
				Degisken = trim(STOK47T.NONSTOK_AD(satirNo)) & " - " & trim(STOK47T.NOTE0(satirNo))
			Else
				Degisken = trim(StokKodu) & "  -  " & trim(StokAdi)
			End if
		Elseif DegiskenAdi = "_SN" then
			res = pe1.SetBold(1)
			Degisken = SiraNo & "."
		else
			Degisken = STOK47T.GetFieldValue(DegiskenAdi,SatirNo)
		end if
		res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
		If Grid(i,4) = "N" Then
			res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
			Ondalik = Grid(i,6)
			Degisken = Doc.NumFormat(Degisken, 20,2)
		ElseIf Grid(i,4) = "D" Then
			Degisken = Doc.StrtoNordate(Degisken)
		End If
		If SutunGenisligi > 0 Then
			res = pe1.write(satirpozr, Sol, satiryuksekligi, SutunGenisligi - 4, Degisken)
			Sol = Sol + SutunGenisligi
		End If	
		Next					 

	sonSatir = pe1.GetBottom()
End Sub
Sub Print_form_sayfasonu(pe1)
	res = pe1.pensize(0.5)
'dýþ çerçeve
	res = pe1.box(UstMargin, 10, AltMargin - UstMargin, SayfaEni - 20)

'dikey çizgiler
	res = pe1.pensize(0.3)
	For i = 0 To UBound(xCizgi)- 1
	  LineX = cint(xCizgi(i))
		If LineX = 0 Then Exit For
		if i <> 0 and i <> 2 then
		  'res = pe1.Line(satirBaslangic - 17, LineX, satirSonu - satirBaslangic + 21, 0)
		end if
	Next
	res = pe1.Line(satirBaslangic - 17, Solmargin+115, satirSonu - satirBaslangic + 25, 0)
	res = pe1.Line(satirBaslangic - 17, Solmargin+155, satirSonu - satirBaslangic + 25, 0)
	res = pe1.Line(satirBaslangic - 17, Solmargin+190, satirSonu - satirBaslangic + 25, 0)
	res = pe1.Line(satirBaslangic - 17, Solmargin+235, satirSonu - satirBaslangic + 25, 0)

	res = pe1.Line(pe1.GetBottom,solMargin,0,sagMargin-solMargin)
	res = pe1.Line(UstMargin + 11,solMargin,0,sagMargin-solMargin)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	For i = 1 to 17
		res = pe1.Line(satirBaslangic + i * satirYuksekligi - 1,solMargin,0,sagMargin-solMargin)	
	Next

	res = pe1.pensize(0.6)
	res = pe1.Line(ustmargin+satiryuksekligi*28,solMargin,0,sagMargin-solMargin)	
	res = pe1.pensize(0.3)

	for i = 1 to STOK47T.Getreccount()
		toplam = toplam + STOK47T.TL_TUTAR(i)
	Next

	res = pe1.write(ustmargin+satiryuksekligi*27,solMargin+157,satiryuksekligi,30,"TOPLAM TUTAR")	
	res = pe1.write(ustmargin+satiryuksekligi*27,solMargin+210,satiryuksekligi,30,doc.numformat(toplam,18,2) & " TL")	

	res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 10)
	res = pe1.Write(satirSonu + 10,solMargin + 5,6,100,"NOTLAR : FÝYATLARA %18 KDV EKLENECEKTÝR.")
	res = pe1.Write(satirSonu + 18,solMargin + 5,6,25,"")
	res = pe1.Write(satirSonu + 18,solMargin + 5,6,100,"Volkan DEMÝREL")
	res = pe1.Write(satirSonu + 23,solMargin + 5,6,100,"Tel: +90(222) 236 16 30 Faks: +90(222) 236 16 29")
	res = pe1.Write(satirSonu + 27,solMargin + 5,6,100,"Mail:  volkandemirel@dundarlarmakina.com")
	res = pe1.Write(satirSonu + 30,solMargin + 5,6,100,"Mail:  satinalma@dundarlarmakina.com")

	res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 8)
	res = pe1.Write(AltMargin + 1,sagMargin - 20,6,20,"FR SA 001 / 2")
End Sub

Sub Alanlar()
	ReDim Grid(7,7)
	Grid(0,0) = "_SN"
	Grid(1,0) = "_CINS"
	Grid(2,0) = "SF_MIKTAR"
	Grid(3,0) = "SF_SF_UNIT"
	Grid(4,0) = "OR_FIYAT"
	Grid(5,0) = "OR_TUTAR"
	Grid(6,0) = "PRICEUNIT"
	Grid(7,0) = "RTALEPEDILENTESTARIH"

	Grid(0,1) = 10
	Grid(1,1) = 105
	Grid(2,1) = 25
	Grid(3,1) = 15
	Grid(4,1) = 35
	Grid(5,1) = 30
	Grid(6,1) = 15
	Grid(7,1) = 40

	Grid(0,2) = ""
	Grid(1,2) = "MALZEME CÝNSÝ"
	Grid(2,2) = "MÝKTAR"
	Grid(3,2) = ""
	Grid(4,2) = "BÝRÝM FÝYAT"
	Grid(5,2) = "TUTAR"
	Grid(6,2) = ""
	Grid(7,2) = "TESLÝM TARÝHÝ"

	
	Grid(0,4) = "C"
	Grid(1,4) = "C"
	Grid(2,4) = "N"
	Grid(3,4) = "C"
	Grid(4,4) = "N"
	Grid(5,4) = "N"
	Grid(6,4) = "C"
	Grid(7,4) = "D"

	For i = 0 To UBound(Grid,1) 
		Degisken = Grid(i,0)
		If Grid(i,4)="N" And Left(degisken,1) <> "_" Then
			Degisken = Grid(i,0)
			ondalik = STOK47T.MaxSigDig(Degisken)	
			If Ondalik > 2 Then Ondalik = 2
			Grid(i,6) = Ondalik		
		End If
	Next	
End Sub
Sub Normal(pe1)
		res = pe1.setbold(0)
		res = pe1.setitalic(0)
		res = pe1.pensize(0.4)
		res = pe1.setbkgcolor(RGB(255, 255, 255))
		res = pe1.setrotation(0)
		res = pe1.settransparentmode(1)
		res = pe1.settextcolor(RGB(0, 0, 0))
		res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 10)
		res = pe1.setalign("left")
End Sub

Sub Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
	ReDim xCizgi(0)

	res = pe1.nopen()
        
	res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 10)
	res = pe1.nopen()
	
	Logo = Doc.Getd7Directory_DataFirm() & trim(Doc.Customp_Gets("!LOGO"))
		
	If logo <> "" Then
		res = pe1.Picture(10, 10, 20, 65, Logo, 0)
		bbb = pe1.GetBottom+1
	End If
	
	res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 20)
	res = pe1.setbold(1)
	res = pe1.setalign("CENTER")
	res = pe1.settransparentmode(1)
	res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop+2, pe1.GetRight+10, 4*satiryuksekligi, 138, "SATINALMA TALEP FORMU" & vbcrlf & "( TEKLÝFLÝ )")
	res = pe1.setalign("LEFT")
	
	Set ID00 = Doc.Select1("ABCST","IDARI00","","",-1)
	IsYeriAdi1 = Trim(ID00.UNVAN_1)
	IsYeriAdi2 = Trim(ID00.UNVAN_2)
	IsYeriAdi = IsYeriAdi1 & " " & IsYeriAdi2

	IsYeriAdres1 = Trim(ID00.ADR_1)
	IsYeriAdres2 = Trim(ID00.ADR_2)
	IsYeriPostaKodu = Trim(ID00.POSTAKODU)
	IsYeriTel = Trim(ID00.TLF1_ALANKODU) & " " & Trim(ID00.TELEFON1)
	IsYeriFax = Trim(ID00.FAX1_ALANKODU) & " " & Trim(ID00.FAX1)	
	email = "satinalma@dundarlarmakina.com"
	
	res = pe1.selectfont("Helvetica", "10")
	aaa = 60
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop-2, pe1.GetRight+1, 10, aaa, IsYeriAdres1 & " " & IsYeriAdres2 & " P.K: " & IsYeriPostaKodu & " ESKÝÞEHÝR" ) 
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetBottom, pe1.GetLeft, 5, aaa, "Tel: " & IsYeriTel & " Fax: " & IsYeriFax) 
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetBottom, pe1.GetLeft, 5, aaa, email) 
	
	res = pe1.pensize(0.5)
	res = pe1.box(10, 10, 20, yataygenislik)
	res = pe1.pensize(0.3)
	res = pe1.selectfont("Helvetica", "10")
	res = pe1.setbold(1)
	res = pe1.setalign("CENTER")
	res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", "10")

	res = pe1.setalign("LEFT")
'*******************
	BBasla = UstMargin + 15
	SatirAra = 6
	Genislik10 = 42
	Genislik11 = 60
	Genislik20 = 25
	Genislik21 = 80	

	FisNo = STOK47E.EVRAKNO(1)
	Tarih = doc.StrtoNordate(STOK47E.TARIH(1))
	TalepEdenBolum = STOK47E.CODEDBOLUM(1)
	TalepEdenBolumAdi = Trim(Doc.GetGecoValue("DEPT","AD",TalepEdenBolum ,-1))
	TalepEdenKisi = STOK47E.CODEDKISI(1)  
	TalepEden = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("PERS00","AD",TalepEdenKisi,"")

	'res = pe1.settransparentmode(0)
	res = pe1.SetBkgColor(RGB(250,250,250))
	'res = pe1.SetBkgColor(RGB(250,000,000))

	res = pe1.setalign("CENTER")
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.settransparentmode(0)
	res = pe1.Line(bbb, 10, 0, SayfaEni-20)
	res = pe1.box(bbb, 10, satiryuksekligi+1, Genislik10)
	res = pe1.settransparentmode(1)
	res = pe1.write(bbb+1, 10, satiryuksekligi, Genislik10, "TALEP EDEN BÖLÜM")
	
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop , pe1.GetRight+1, satiryuksekligi, Genislik11, Trim(TalepEdenBolumAdi))

	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.settransparentmode(0)
	res = pe1.box(bbb, pe1.GetRight+1, satiryuksekligi+1, Genislik10-7)
	res = pe1.settransparentmode(1)
	res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop+1, pe1.GetLeft, satiryuksekligi, Genislik10-7, "TALEP EDEN KÝÞÝ")

	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight+1, satiryuksekligi, Genislik11, Trim(TalepEden))		
	
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.settransparentmode(0)
	res = pe1.box(bbb, pe1.GetRight+1, satiryuksekligi+1, Genislik10-18)
	res = pe1.settransparentmode(1)	
	res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop+1, pe1.GetLeft, satiryuksekligi, Genislik10-18, "TALEP NO")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight+1, satiryuksekligi, 15, Trim(FisNo))				
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)

	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.settransparentmode(0)
	res = pe1.box(bbb, pe1.GetRight+1, satiryuksekligi+1, 15)
	res = pe1.settransparentmode(1)	
	res = pe1.write(pe1.getTop+1 , pe1.GetLeft, satiryuksekligi, 14, "TARÝH")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.write(pe1.getTop , pe1.GetRight+1, satiryuksekligi, 20, Tarih)
	res = pe1.Line(pe1.GetTop-1, pe1.GetRight, satiryuksekligi+1, 0)
	res = pe1.pensize(0.5)
	res = pe1.Box(bbb, 10, satiryuksekligi+1, SayfaEni-20)
	res = pe1.pensize(0.3)
	KolonBaslikBaslangic = pe1.GetBottom+2	
	'***********************		
	res = pe1.settransparentmode(0)
	res = pe1.pensize(0.5)
	res = pe1.box(KolonBaslikBaslangic-1, 10, 2*satiryuksekligi+1,  SayfaEni - 20)
	res = pe1.pensize(0.3)
	res = pe1.settransparentmode(1)
	
	res = pe1.setalign("CENTER")
	res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 10)
	Sol = solmargin+1

	For i = 0 To UBound(Grid,1)
		SutunGenisligi = Grid(i,1)
		Baslik = Grid(i,2)
		If SutunGenisligi > 0 Then	
			If Baslik <> "Para Birimi" Then 
				If Baslik = "Birim Fiyat" Or Baslik = "Tutar" Then 
					SutunGenisligi = Grid(10,1)+Grid(13,1)
				Else
					SutunGenisligi = Grid(i,1)
				End If
				
				res = pe1.SetBold(1)
				res = pe1.write(KolonBaslikBaslangic, Sol, 2*satiryuksekligi, SutunGenisligi , Baslik)
				res = pe1.SetBold(0)
				res = pe1.Line(pe1.GetTop-1, pe1.GetRight, 2*satiryuksekligi+1, 0)	
				
				Sol = Sol + SutunGenisligi
			End If
		End If
	Next
	satirBaslangic = pe1.GetBottom
End Sub
'----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Sub Print_form_satir(pe1, satirNo)

	If Trim(STOK47T.KOD(satirNo)) = "" Then Exit Sub
	SiraNo = SiraNo + 1
	sonSatir = pe1.GetBottom()
	If sonSatir >= satirSonu Then
		Call Print_form_sayfasonu(pe1)
		res = pe1.PageBreak()
		Call Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
		satirpozr = satirBaslangic
	Else
		If satirNo = 1 Then
			satirpozr = satirBaslangic+1
		Else
			satirpozr = pe1.GetTop()+2
		End If
	End If

	res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 10)
	res = pe1.setbold(0)
	Sol = solMargin+1.5
	res = pe1.setalign("LEFT")
	
	For i=0 To UBound(Grid,1)
		SutunGenisligi = Grid(i,1)
		DegiskenAdi = Grid(i,0)
		
		res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	
		If DegiskenAdi = "_SN" Then
			res = pe1.SetBold(0)
			Degisken = SiraNo 
		Else
			Degisken = Trim(cstr(STOK47T.GetFieldValue(DegiskenAdi,SatirNo)))
		End If
		
		res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")

		res = pe1.settransparentmode(1)

		If Grid(i,4) = "N" Then
			'res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
			'Ondalik = Grid(i,6)
			'Degisken = Doc.NumFormat(Degisken, 20,2)
		ElseIf Grid(i,4) = "D" Then
			Degisken = Doc.StrtoNordate(Degisken)
		End If

		If  DegiskenAdi = "_a" Or DegiskenAdi = "_b" or DegiskenAdi = "BIRIMFIYAT" or DegiskenAdi = "TUTAR" OR DegiskenAdi = "ONAYKUTUSU" OR DegiskenAdi = "PARABIRIMI" Then SutunGenisligi=0
				
		'If SutunGenisligi > 0 Then
			If DegiskenAdi = "TEKLIFALINANFIRMALAR" Then
				If Degisken <> "PARABIRIMI" and Degisken <> "ONAYKUTUSU" Then
					Set KTTS47T = Doc.Select1("vvvccc", "KTT_S47T", "EVRAKNO='"&STOK47T.EVRAKNO(satirNo)&"' & OR_TRNUM='"&STOK47T.SRNUM(satirNo)&"'", "", -1)
					KayitSayisi = KTTS47T.GetRecCount
					If KayitSayisi = 0 Then 
						satirpozr = satirpozr + satiryuksekligi + 1
					Else
						For j = 1 To KayitSayisi
							res = pe1.setalign("LEFT")
							'Teklif Alýnan Firmalar
							pay = 0.5
							If j >1 Then res=pe1.Line(satirpozr, Sol-0.5, satiryuksekligi+1, 0)
							res = pe1.write(satirpozr+pay, Sol, satiryuksekligi, SutunGenisligi-1, Trim(KTTS47T.ALAN2(j)))
							
							TeklifSolKonum = pe1.GetLeft
							res = pe1.Line(pe1.GetTop-pay,pe1.GetRight+0.5, satiryuksekligi+1, 0)		 
								
							Sol = Sol + Grid(7,1)
							res = pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")
							'Ödeme Þekli
							res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop+pay, Sol, satiryuksekligi, Grid(8,1)-1, Trim(KTTS47T.ALAN5(j)))
							res = pe1.Line(pe1.GetTop-pay, pe1.GetRight+0.5, satiryuksekligi+1, 0)

							Sol = Sol + Grid(8,1)
							res = pe1.setalign("RIGHT")
							'Vade
							res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop+pay, Sol, satiryuksekligi, Grid(9,1)-1, Trim(KTTS47T.ALAN6(j)))
							res = pe1.Line(pe1.GetTop-pay, pe1.GetRight+0.5, satiryuksekligi+1, 0)
							
							Sol = Sol + Grid(9,1)
							'If DegiskenAdi = "BIRIMFIYAT" Or DegiskenAdi = "TUTAR" Then SutunGenisligi = Grid(10,1)+Grid(13,1)
							'B.Fiyat
							res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop+pay, Sol, satiryuksekligi, Grid(10,1)+Grid(13,1)-1, Doc.NumFormat(KTTS47T.ALAN7(j), 20,2) & " " & KTTS47T.ALAN8(j))
							res = pe1.Line(pe1.GetTop-pay, pe1.GetRight+0.5, satiryuksekligi+1, 0)
			
							Sol = Sol + Grid(10,1)+Grid(13,1)
							'Tutar
							res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop+pay, Sol, satiryuksekligi, Grid(10,1)+Grid(13,1)-1, Doc.NumFormat(KTTS47T.ALAN10(j),20,2) & " " & KTTS47T.ALAN8(j))
							res = pe1.Line(pe1.GetTop-pay, pe1.GetRight+0.5, satiryuksekligi+1, 0)
		
							Sol = Sol + Grid(10,1)+Grid(13,1)
							res = pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")
							'Onay
							res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop+pay, Sol, satiryuksekligi, Grid(12,1)-0.5, "[   ]")
							res = pe1.Line(pe1.GetTop-pay, pe1.GetRight, satiryuksekligi+1, 0)
							
							Sol = TeklifSolKonum
							satirpozr = satirpozr + satiryuksekligi + 1
							If j = 1 Then
								res = pe1.Line(pe1.GetBottom, 10, 0, SayfaEni-20)
							Else
								res = pe1.Line(pe1.GetBottom, TeklifSolKonum-0.5, 0, SayfaEni-TeklifSolKonum-9.5)
							End If
							
							sonSatir = pe1.GetBottom+5
							If sonSatir >= satirSonu Then
								Call Print_form_sayfasonu(pe1)
								res = pe1.PageBreak()
								Call Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
								satirpozr = satirBaslangic+1
							End If
						Next
					End If
				End If
			Else	
				If DegiskenAdi = "RTALEPEDILENTESTARIH" Then res = pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")
				If DegiskenAdi = "SF_MIKTAR" Then 
					res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
					Degisken = Doc.NumFormat(Degisken,20,2)
				End If
				If DegiskenAdi = "_SN" Then res = pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")
				If DegiskenAdi = "SF_SF_UNIT" Then res = pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")

				If DegiskenAdi = "KOD" Then 
					'If Trim(cstr(STOK47T.GetFieldValue("STOK_NONSTOK",SatirNo))) = "NS" Then Degisken = ""
				ElseIf DegiskenAdi = "KOD_STOK00_AD" Then
					If Trim(cstr(STOK47T.GetFieldValue("STOK_NONSTOK",SatirNo))) = "NS" Then 
						Degisken = Trim(cstr(STOK47T.GetFieldValue("NONSTOK_AD",SatirNo)))
					End If
				End If
				
				res = pe1.Line(satirpozr, 10, 0, SayfaEni-20)
				
				res = pe1.write(satirpozr+pay+0.5, Sol, satiryuksekligi, SutunGenisligi-1, Degisken)
				
				If SutunGenisligi > 0 Then res = pe1.Line(pe1.GetTop-pay-0.5, pe1.GetRight+0.5, satiryuksekligi+1, 0)	
				If DegiskenAdi = "_SN" Then res = pe1.Line(pe1.GetTop, 10, satiryuksekligi+1, 0)	
				
			End If
			If DegiskenAdi = "BIRIMFIYAT" Or DegiskenAdi = "TUTAR" Then 
				SutunGenisligi = Grid(10,1)+Grid(13,1)
			Else
				SutunGenisligi = Grid(i,1)
			End If
			Sol = Sol + SutunGenisligi
		'End If	
		res = pe1.pensize(0.3)
	Next		
	sonSatir = pe1.GetBottom()
End Sub
'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Sub Print_form_sayfasonu(pe1)
	'dýþ çerçeve
	res = pe1.pensize(0.5)
	res = pe1.settransparentmode(1)
	res = pe1.box(SatirBaslangic+1, 10, satirSonu-SatirBaslangic, SayfaEni - 20)
	res = pe1.pensize(0.3)	 

	res = pe1.SetBold(1)

	AltBilgiSatirSayisi = 5.5
	res = pe1.pensize(0.5)
	res = pe1.settransparentmode(1)
	res = pe1.box(satirSonu+2, 10, AltBilgiSatirSayisi*satiryuksekligi, 100)
	yyy = pe1.GetRight
	uuu = pe1.GetTop
	
	res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 10)
	res = pe1.Write(satirSonu+3, solMargin+1, satiryuksekligi, 25, "Notlar :")

	res = pe1.settransparentmode(1)
	res = pe1.box(uuu, yyy, AltBilgiSatirSayisi*satiryuksekligi, 100)
	zzz = pe1.GetRight
	vvv = pe1.GetTop
	
	res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 10)
	res = pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")
	res = pe1.settransparentmode(0)
	res = pe1.Box(uuu, yyy, satiryuksekligi, 100)
	res = pe1.settransparentmode(1)
	res = pe1.Write(uuu+0.5, yyy, satiryuksekligi, 100, "TEKLÝFÝ OLUÞTURAN")
	
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetBottom, yyy, satiryuksekligi, 23, " ADI SOYADI")
	res = pe1.Line(pe1.GetTop-0.5, pe1.GetRight+0.5, satiryuksekligi, 0)	
	TeklifiOlusturan = Trim(Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK47E", "ELOG_USERNAME", Trim(FisNo), ""))
	PersonelKodu = Trim(Doc.GetDBFieldValue("UDEF", "PERSONELKODU", TeklifiOlusturan, ""))
	PersonelAdi = Trim(Doc.GetDBFieldValue("PERS00", "AD", PersonelKodu, ""))
	
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop+0.5, pe1.GetRight+1, satiryuksekligi, 50, PersonelAdi)
	
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetBottom-0.5, yyy, satiryuksekligi, 23, " TARIH")
	res = pe1.Line(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight+0.5, satiryuksekligi, 0)	
		res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight+1, satiryuksekligi, 50, TARIH)
	
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetBottom+0.5*satiryuksekligi, yyy, satiryuksekligi, 23, " IMZA")
	res = pe1.Line(pe1.GetTop-2.5, pe1.GetRight+0.5, 2.5*satiryuksekligi, 0)	

	res = pe1.settransparentmode(1)
	res = pe1.box(vvv, zzz, AltBilgiSatirSayisi*satiryuksekligi, 77)

	hhh = pe1.GetBottom
	
	res = pe1.settransparentmode(0)
	res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 10)
	res = pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")
	res = pe1.settransparentmode(0)
	res = pe1.Box(vvv, zzz, satiryuksekligi, 77)
	res = pe1.settransparentmode(1)
	res = pe1.Write(vvv+0.5, zzz, satiryuksekligi, 77, "TEKLÝFÝ ONAYLAYAN")

	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetBottom, zzz, satiryuksekligi, 77, " GENEL MÜDÜR")

	res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 8)
	res = pe1.Write(hhh+1, sagMargin-20, satiryuksekligi,20, "FR xxx / yy")
End Sub
'--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Sub Alanlar()
	ReDim Grid(15,7)
	Grid(0,0) = "_SN"
	Grid(1,0) = "KOD"
	Grid(2,0) = "KOD_STOK00_AD"
	Grid(3,0) = "NOTE1"
	Grid(4,0) = "RTALEPEDILENTESTARIH"
	Grid(5,0) = "SF_MIKTAR"
	Grid(6,0) = "SF_SF_UNIT"
	Grid(7,0) = "TEKLIFALINANFIRMALAR"
	Grid(8,0) = "_a"
	Grid(9,0) = "_b"
	Grid(10,0) = "BIRIMFIYAT"
	Grid(11,0) = "TUTAR"
	Grid(12,0) = "ONAYKUTUSU"
	Grid(13,0) = "PARABIRIMI"
	Grid(14,0) = "STOK_NONSTOK"
	Grid(15,0) = "NONSTOK_AD"
	
	Grid(0,1) = 8
	Grid(1,1) = 30
	Grid(2,1) = 40
	Grid(3,1) = 35
	Grid(4,1) = 21
	Grid(5,1) = 17
	Grid(6,1) = 11
	Grid(7,1) = 30
	Grid(8,1) = 14
	Grid(9,1) = 12
	Grid(10,1) = 20
	Grid(11,1) = 20
	Grid(12,1) = 12
	Grid(13,1) = 3
	Grid(14,1) = 0
	Grid(15,1) = 0
	

	Grid(0,2) = "S. No"
	Grid(1,2) = "Malzeme Kodu"
	Grid(2,2) = "Malzeme Adý"
	Grid(3,2) = "Ne Ýçin Talep Ediliyor"
	Grid(4,2) = "Teslim Tarihi"
	Grid(5,2) = "Miktar"
	Grid(6,2) = "Birim"
	Grid(7,2) = "Teklif Alýnan Firma/lar"
	Grid(8,2) = "Ödeme Þekli"
	Grid(9,2) = "Vade"
	Grid(10,2) = "Birim Fiyat"
	Grid(11,2) = "Tutar"
	Grid(12,2) = "Onay"
	Grid(13,2) = "Para Birimi"
	Grid(14,2) = "NS"
	Grid(15,2) = "NS Açýklama"

	Grid(0,4) = "N"
	Grid(1,4) = "C"
	Grid(2,4) = "C"
	Grid(3,4) = "C"
	Grid(4,4) = "D"
	Grid(5,4) = "N"
	Grid(6,4) = "C"
	Grid(7,4) = "C"
	Grid(8,4) = "C"
	Grid(9,4) = "N"
	Grid(10,4) = "N"
	Grid(11,4) = "N"
	Grid(12,4) = "C"
	Grid(13,4) = "C"
	Grid(14,4) = "C"
	Grid(15,4) = "C"

	For i = 0 To UBound(Grid,1) 
		Degisken = Grid(i,0)
		If Grid(i,4)="N" And Left(degisken,1) <> "_" Then
			Degisken = Grid(i,0)
			ondalik = STOK47T.MaxSigDig(Degisken)	
			If Ondalik > 2 Then Ondalik = 2
			Grid(i,6) = Ondalik		
		End If
	Next	
End Sub

Form Kaynak Kodu

Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için : 
Açýklama : Satýnalma Teklif Ýsteme Formu
Kod : 
Public sayfaTuru, sayfaBoyu, sayfaEni, basla, bitir, sonSatir, satirSonu, ToplamEn , BasilabilirAlan, ttt, kkk
Public siraNo, satirBaslangic,UstMargin, AltMargin, sagMargin, solMargin, satirYuksekligi, yataygenislik
Public AlanSayisi()	,FisNo, Tarih, aaa
Public Grid

Sub PrintForm()
	ToplamEn = 0
	BasilabilirAlan = 0
	siraNo = 0

	sayfaBoyu = 210
	sayfaEni = 297
	UstMargin = 30
	AltMargin = sayfaBoyu - 15
	solMargin = 10
	sagMargin = sayfaEni - 10
	yataygenislik = sayfaEni - 10 - solMargin
	satiryuksekligi = 5
	SiraNo = 0
	Set pe1 = doc.CreatePrintEngineOutput()

	Call Alanlar()
	res = pe1.Open(0, "Satýnalma Talebi", SayfaEni ,SayfaBoyu,"LANDSCAPE")

	BasilabilirAlan = SayfaEni - 20
	satirSonu = sayfaBoyu - 50

	If doc.ErrorNumber Then
		MsgBox ("Hata main : " & doc.ErrorNumber)
		Exit Sub
	End If
	
	Dim satirSayisi_tableT
	satirSayisi_tableT = STOK47T.GetRecCount()
	Call Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
	IlkSayfa = pe1.GetPageCount
	For i = 1 To satirSayisi_tableT
		Call Print_form_satir(pe1, i)
	Next
		
	Call Print_form_sayfasonu(pe1)

	SonSayfa = pe1.GetPageCount
	Call SayfaSayisiYazdir(pe1, Ilksayfa, SonSayfa)

	res = pe1.Show()
	res = Doc.ActivateAfterMacro(1)
End Sub
'----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Sub Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
	ReDim xCizgi(0)

	res = pe1.nopen()
        
	res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 10)
	res = pe1.nopen()
	
	Logo = Doc.Getd7Directory_DataFirm() & trim(Doc.Customp_Gets("!LOGO1"))
	res = pe1.setalign("CENTER")
	res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 12)
	res = pe1.setbold(1)
	res = pe1.pensize(0.3)
	res = pe1.write(10, solMargin, satiryuksekligi, yataygenislik, "DÜNDARLAR MAKÝNA SAN. VE TÝC.LTD.ÞTÝ. KALÝTE YÖNETÝM SÝSTEMÝ")
	res = pe1.Line(pe1.GetBottom, solMargin, 0, yataygenislik)
	res = pe1.nopen()
	res = pe1.Picture(pe1.GetBottom+3, solMargin+5, 20, 20, Logo, 0)
	res = pe1.pensize(0.3)
	res = pe1.Line(pe1.GetTop-3, pe1.GetRight+5, 26, 0)
	
	res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 20)
	res = pe1.setbold(1)
	res = pe1.setalign("CENTER")
	
	'res = pe1.settransparentmode(0)
	res = pe1.SetBkgColor(RGB(250,250,250))

	res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop+6, pe1.GetRight+1, 3.5*satiryuksekligi, yataygenislik-85, "SATINALMA TEKLÝF ÝSTEME FORMU")

	res = pe1.setalign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.Line(pe1.GetTop-6, pe1.GetRight+1, 26, 0)
	azx = pe1.GetRight
	
	Set ID00 = Doc.Select1("ABCST","IDARI00","","",-1)
	IsYeriAdi1 = Trim(ID00.UNVAN_1)
	IsYeriAdi2 = Trim(ID00.UNVAN_2)
	IsYeriAdi = IsYeriAdi1 & " " & IsYeriAdi2

	IsYeriAdres1 = Trim(ID00.ADR_1)
	IsYeriAdres2 = Trim(ID00.ADR_2)
	IsYeriPostaKodu = Trim(ID00.POSTAKODU)
	IsYeriTel = Trim(ID00.TLF1_ALANKODU) & " " & Trim(ID00.TELEFON1)
	IsYeriFax = Trim(ID00.FAX1_ALANKODU) & " " & Trim(ID00.FAX1)	
	email = "satinalma@dundarlarmakina.com"
	
	res = pe1.selectfont("Helvetica", "10")
	aaa = 28
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop+1, azx+1, satiryuksekligi, aaa, "Doküman Kodu") 
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight+1, satiryuksekligi, aaa-8, ": FRM.06") 
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetBottom, azx+1, satiryuksekligi, aaa, "Ýlk Yayýn Tarihi") 
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight+1, satiryuksekligi, aaa-8, ": 01.01.2014") 
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetBottom, azx+1, satiryuksekligi, aaa, "Revizyon No") 
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight+1, satiryuksekligi, aaa-8, ": 00") 
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetBottom, azx+1, satiryuksekligi, aaa, "Revizyon Tarihi") 
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight+1, satiryuksekligi, aaa-8, ": 00") 
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetBottom, azx+1, satiryuksekligi, aaa, "Sayfa No") 
	ttt = pe1.GetTop
	kkk = pe1.GetRight+1
	'res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight+1, satiryuksekligi, aaa-8, ": 1/1") 
			
	res = pe1.pensize(0.3)
	res = pe1.settransparentmode(1)
	res = pe1.box(10, 10, 31, yataygenislik)
	res = pe1.pensize(0.3)
	res = pe1.selectfont("Helvetica", "10")
	res = pe1.setbold(1)
	res = pe1.setalign("CENTER")
	res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", "10")
	bbb = pe1.GetBottom+1
	res = pe1.setalign("LEFT")
'*******************
	BBasla = UstMargin + 15
	SatirAra = 6
	Genislik10 = 42
	Genislik11 = 60
	Genislik20 = 25
	Genislik21 = 80	

	FisNo = STOK47E.EVRAKNO(1)
	Tarih = doc.StrtoNordate(STOK47E.TARIH(1))
	TalepEdenBolum = STOK47E.CODEDBOLUM(1)
	TalepEdenBolumAdi = Trim(Doc.GetGecoValue("DEPT","AD",TalepEdenBolum ,-1))
	TalepEdenKisi = STOK47E.CODEDKISI(1)  
	TalepEden = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("PERS00","AD",TalepEdenKisi,"")

	res = pe1.setalign("CENTER")
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.settransparentmode(0)
	res = pe1.Line(bbb, 10, 0, SayfaEni-20)
	res = pe1.box(bbb, 10, satiryuksekligi+1, Genislik10)
	res = pe1.settransparentmode(1)
	res = pe1.write(bbb+1, 10, satiryuksekligi, Genislik10, "TALEP EDEN BÖLÜM")
	
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop , pe1.GetRight+1, satiryuksekligi, Genislik11, Trim(TalepEdenBolumAdi))

	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.settransparentmode(0)
	res = pe1.box(bbb, pe1.GetRight+1, satiryuksekligi+1, Genislik10-7)
	res = pe1.settransparentmode(1)
	res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop+1, pe1.GetLeft, satiryuksekligi, Genislik10-7, "TALEP EDEN KÝÞÝ")

	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight+1, satiryuksekligi, Genislik11, Trim(TalepEden))		
	
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.settransparentmode(0)
	res = pe1.box(bbb, pe1.GetRight+1, satiryuksekligi+1, Genislik10-18)
	res = pe1.settransparentmode(1)	
	res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop+1, pe1.GetLeft, satiryuksekligi, Genislik10-18, "TALEP NO")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight+1, satiryuksekligi, 15, Trim(FisNo))				
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)

	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.settransparentmode(0)
	res = pe1.box(bbb, pe1.GetRight+1, satiryuksekligi+1, 15)
	res = pe1.settransparentmode(1)	
	res = pe1.write(pe1.getTop+1 , pe1.GetLeft, satiryuksekligi, 14, "TARÝH")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.write(pe1.getTop , pe1.GetRight+1, satiryuksekligi, 20, Tarih)
	res = pe1.Line(pe1.GetTop-1, pe1.GetRight, satiryuksekligi+1, 0)
	res = pe1.pensize(0.3)
	res = pe1.Box(bbb, 10, satiryuksekligi+1, SayfaEni-20)
	res = pe1.pensize(0.3)
	KolonBaslikBaslangic = pe1.GetBottom+2	
	'***********************		
	res = pe1.settransparentmode(0)
	res = pe1.pensize(0.3)
	res = pe1.box(KolonBaslikBaslangic-1, 10, 2*satiryuksekligi+1,  SayfaEni - 20)
	res = pe1.pensize(0.3)
	res = pe1.settransparentmode(1)
	
	res = pe1.setalign("CENTER")
	res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 10)
	Sol = solmargin+1

	For i = 0 To UBound(Grid,1)
		SutunGenisligi = Grid(i,1)
		Baslik = Grid(i,2)
		If SutunGenisligi > 0 Then	
			If Baslik <> "Para Birimi" Then 
				If Baslik = "Birim Fiyat" Or Baslik = "Tutar" Then 
					SutunGenisligi = Grid(10,1)+Grid(13,1)
				Else
					SutunGenisligi = Grid(i,1)
				End If
				
				res = pe1.SetBold(1)
				res = pe1.write(KolonBaslikBaslangic, Sol, 2*satiryuksekligi, SutunGenisligi , Baslik)
				res = pe1.SetBold(0)
				res = pe1.Line(pe1.GetTop-1, pe1.GetRight, 2*satiryuksekligi+1, 0)	
				
				Sol = Sol + SutunGenisligi
			End If
		End If
	Next
	satirBaslangic = pe1.GetBottom
End Sub
'----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Sub Print_form_satir(pe1, satirNo)

	If Trim(STOK47T.KOD(satirNo)) = "" Then Exit Sub
	SiraNo = SiraNo + 1
	sonSatir = pe1.GetBottom()
	If sonSatir >= satirSonu Then
		Call Print_form_sayfasonu(pe1)
		res = pe1.PageBreak()
		Call Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
		satirpozr = satirBaslangic
	Else
		If satirNo = 1 Then
			satirpozr = satirBaslangic+1
		Else
			satirpozr = pe1.GetTop()+2
		End If
	End If

	res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 10)
	res = pe1.setbold(0)
	Sol = solMargin+1.5
	res = pe1.setalign("LEFT")
	
	For i=0 To UBound(Grid,1)
		SutunGenisligi = Grid(i,1)
		DegiskenAdi = Grid(i,0)
		
		res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	
		If DegiskenAdi = "_SN" Then
			res = pe1.SetBold(0)
			Degisken = SiraNo 
		Else
			Degisken = Trim(cstr(STOK47T.GetFieldValue(DegiskenAdi,SatirNo)))
		End If
		
		res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")

		res = pe1.settransparentmode(1)

		If Grid(i,4) = "N" Then
			'res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
			'Ondalik = Grid(i,6)
			'Degisken = Doc.NumFormat(Degisken, 20,2)
		ElseIf Grid(i,4) = "D" Then
			Degisken = Doc.StrtoNordate(Degisken)
		End If

		If  DegiskenAdi = "_a" Or DegiskenAdi = "_b" or DegiskenAdi = "BIRIMFIYAT" or DegiskenAdi = "TUTAR" OR DegiskenAdi = "ONAYKUTUSU" OR DegiskenAdi = "PARABIRIMI" Then SutunGenisligi=0
				
		'If SutunGenisligi > 0 Then
			If DegiskenAdi = "TEKLIFALINANFIRMALAR" Then
				If Degisken <> "PARABIRIMI" and Degisken <> "ONAYKUTUSU" Then
					Set KTTS47T = Doc.Select1("vvvccc", "KTT_S47T", "EVRAKNO='"&STOK47T.EVRAKNO(satirNo)&"' & OR_TRNUM='"&STOK47T.TRNUM(satirNo)&"'", "", -1)
					KayitSayisi = KTTS47T.GetRecCount
					If KayitSayisi = 0 Then 
						satirpozr = satirpozr + satiryuksekligi + 1
					Else
						For j = 1 To KayitSayisi
							res = pe1.setalign("LEFT")
							'Teklif Alýnan Firmalar
							pay = 0.5
							If j >1 Then res=pe1.Line(satirpozr, Sol-0.5, satiryuksekligi+1, 0)
							res = pe1.write(satirpozr+pay, Sol, satiryuksekligi, SutunGenisligi-1, Trim(KTTS47T.ALAN2(j)))
							
							TeklifSolKonum = pe1.GetLeft
							res = pe1.Line(pe1.GetTop-pay,pe1.GetRight+0.5, satiryuksekligi+1, 0)		 
								
							Sol = Sol + Grid(7,1)
							res = pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")
							'Ödeme Þekli
							res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop+pay, Sol, satiryuksekligi, Grid(8,1)-1, Trim(KTTS47T.ALAN5(j)))
							res = pe1.Line(pe1.GetTop-pay, pe1.GetRight+0.5, satiryuksekligi+1, 0)

							Sol = Sol + Grid(8,1)
							res = pe1.setalign("RIGHT")
							'Vade
							res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop+pay, Sol, satiryuksekligi, Grid(9,1)-1, Trim(KTTS47T.ALAN6(j)))
							res = pe1.Line(pe1.GetTop-pay, pe1.GetRight+0.5, satiryuksekligi+1, 0)
							
							Sol = Sol + Grid(9,1)
							'If DegiskenAdi = "BIRIMFIYAT" Or DegiskenAdi = "TUTAR" Then SutunGenisligi = Grid(10,1)+Grid(13,1)
							'B.Fiyat
							res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop+pay, Sol, satiryuksekligi, Grid(10,1)+Grid(13,1)-1, Doc.NumFormat(KTTS47T.ALAN7(j), 20,2) & " " & KTTS47T.ALAN8(j))
							res = pe1.Line(pe1.GetTop-pay, pe1.GetRight+0.5, satiryuksekligi+1, 0)
			
							Sol = Sol + Grid(10,1)+Grid(13,1)
							'Tutar
							res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop+pay, Sol, satiryuksekligi, Grid(10,1)+Grid(13,1)-1, Doc.NumFormat(KTTS47T.ALAN10(j),20,2) & " " & KTTS47T.ALAN8(j))
							res = pe1.Line(pe1.GetTop-pay, pe1.GetRight+0.5, satiryuksekligi+1, 0)
		
							Sol = Sol + Grid(10,1)+Grid(13,1)
							res = pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")
							'Onay
							res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop+pay, Sol, satiryuksekligi, Grid(12,1)-0.5, "[   ]")
							res = pe1.Line(pe1.GetTop-pay, pe1.GetRight, satiryuksekligi+1, 0)
							
							Sol = TeklifSolKonum
							satirpozr = satirpozr + satiryuksekligi + 1
							If j = 1 Then
								res = pe1.Line(pe1.GetBottom, 10, 0, SayfaEni-20)
							Else
								res = pe1.Line(pe1.GetBottom, TeklifSolKonum-0.5, 0, SayfaEni-TeklifSolKonum-9.5)
							End If
							
							sonSatir = pe1.GetBottom+5
							If sonSatir >= satirSonu Then
								Call Print_form_sayfasonu(pe1)
								res = pe1.PageBreak()
								Call Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
								satirpozr = satirBaslangic+1
							End If
						Next
					End If
				End If
			Else	
				If DegiskenAdi = "RTALEPEDILENTESTARIH" Then res = pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")
				If DegiskenAdi = "SF_MIKTAR" Then 
					res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
					Degisken = Doc.NumFormat(Degisken,20,2)
				End If
				If DegiskenAdi = "_SN" Then res = pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")
				If DegiskenAdi = "SF_SF_UNIT" Then res = pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")

				If DegiskenAdi = "KOD" Then 
					'If Trim(cstr(STOK47T.GetFieldValue("STOK_NONSTOK",SatirNo))) = "NS" Then Degisken = ""
				ElseIf DegiskenAdi = "KOD_STOK00_AD" Then
					If Trim(cstr(STOK47T.GetFieldValue("STOK_NONSTOK",SatirNo))) = "NS" Then 
						Degisken = Trim(cstr(STOK47T.GetFieldValue("NONSTOK_AD",SatirNo)))
					End If
				End If
				
				res = pe1.Line(satirpozr, 10, 0, SayfaEni-20)
				
				res = pe1.write(satirpozr+pay+0.5, Sol, satiryuksekligi, SutunGenisligi-1, Degisken)
				
				If SutunGenisligi > 0 Then res = pe1.Line(pe1.GetTop-pay-0.5, pe1.GetRight+0.5, satiryuksekligi+1, 0)	
				If DegiskenAdi = "_SN" Then res = pe1.Line(pe1.GetTop, 10, satiryuksekligi+1, 0)	
				
			End If
			If DegiskenAdi = "BIRIMFIYAT" Or DegiskenAdi = "TUTAR" Then 
				SutunGenisligi = Grid(10,1)+Grid(13,1)
			Else
				SutunGenisligi = Grid(i,1)
			End If
			Sol = Sol + SutunGenisligi
		'End If	
		res = pe1.pensize(0.3)
	Next		
	sonSatir = pe1.GetBottom()
End Sub
'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Sub Print_form_sayfasonu(pe1)
	'dýþ çerçeve
	res = pe1.pensize(0.3)
	res = pe1.settransparentmode(1)
	res = pe1.box(SatirBaslangic+1, 10, satirSonu-SatirBaslangic, SayfaEni - 20)
	res = pe1.pensize(0.3)	 

	res = pe1.SetBold(1)

	AltBilgiSatirSayisi = 5.5
	res = pe1.pensize(0.3)
	res = pe1.settransparentmode(1)
	res = pe1.box(satirSonu+2, 10, AltBilgiSatirSayisi*satiryuksekligi, 100)
	yyy = pe1.GetRight
	uuu = pe1.GetTop
	
	res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 10)
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.Write(satirSonu+3, solMargin+1, satiryuksekligi, 25, "Notlar :")

	res = pe1.settransparentmode(1)
	res = pe1.box(uuu, yyy, AltBilgiSatirSayisi*satiryuksekligi, 100)
	zzz = pe1.GetRight
	vvv = pe1.GetTop
	
	res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 10)
	res = pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")
	res = pe1.settransparentmode(0)
	res = pe1.Box(uuu, yyy, satiryuksekligi, 100)
	res = pe1.settransparentmode(1)
	res = pe1.Write(uuu+0.5, yyy, satiryuksekligi, 100, "TEKLÝFÝ ÝSTEYEN")
	
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetBottom, yyy, satiryuksekligi, 23, " ADI SOYADI")
	res = pe1.Line(pe1.GetTop-0.5, pe1.GetRight+0.5, satiryuksekligi, 0)	
	TeklifiOlusturan = Trim(Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK47E", "ELOG_USERNAME", Trim(FisNo), ""))
	PersonelKodu = Trim(Doc.GetDBFieldValue("UDEF", "PERSONELKODU", TeklifiOlusturan, ""))
	PersonelAdi = Trim(Doc.GetDBFieldValue("PERS00", "AD", PersonelKodu, ""))
	
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop+0.5, pe1.GetRight+1, satiryuksekligi, 50, PersonelAdi)
	
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetBottom-0.5, yyy, satiryuksekligi, 23, " TARIH")
	res = pe1.Line(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight+0.5, satiryuksekligi, 0)	
		res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight+1, satiryuksekligi, 50, TARIH)
	
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetBottom+0.5*satiryuksekligi, yyy, satiryuksekligi, 23, " IMZA")
	res = pe1.Line(pe1.GetTop-2.5, pe1.GetRight+0.5, 2.5*satiryuksekligi, 0)	

	res = pe1.settransparentmode(1)
	res = pe1.box(vvv, zzz, AltBilgiSatirSayisi*satiryuksekligi, 77)

	hhh = pe1.GetBottom
	
	res = pe1.settransparentmode(0)
	res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 10)
	res = pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")
	res = pe1.settransparentmode(0)
	res = pe1.Box(vvv, zzz, satiryuksekligi, 77)
	res = pe1.settransparentmode(1)
	res = pe1.Write(vvv+0.5, zzz, satiryuksekligi, 77, "TEKLÝFÝ HAZIRLAYAN")

	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetBottom, zzz, satiryuksekligi, 77, " SATIÞ SORUMLUSU")

	res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 10)
	res = pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")
	res = pe1.Write(hhh+1, SolMargin, satiryuksekligi,yataygenislik, "Organize Sanayi Bölgesi 23. Cadde No: 3  Pk:26110 Odunpazarý / Eskiþehir Tel:+90(222)236 16 30 Fks:+90(222) 236 16 31 ")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetBottom, pe1.GetLeft, satiryuksekligi,yataygenislik , "Volkan DEMÝREL volkandemirel@dundarlarmakina.com  / Ýbrahim BAL satinalma@dundarlarmakina.com ")
	res = pe1.Box(hhh+1, SolMargin, 2*satiryuksekligi, yataygenislik)
End Sub
'--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Sub Alanlar()
	ReDim Grid(15,7)
	Grid(0,0) = "_SN"
	Grid(1,0) = "KOD"
	Grid(2,0) = "KOD_STOK00_AD"
	Grid(3,0) = "NOTE1"
	Grid(4,0) = "RTALEPEDILENTESTARIH"
	Grid(5,0) = "SF_MIKTAR"
	Grid(6,0) = "SF_SF_UNIT"
	Grid(7,0) = "TEKLIFALINANFIRMALAR"
	Grid(8,0) = "_a"
	Grid(9,0) = "_b"
	Grid(10,0) = "BIRIMFIYAT"
	Grid(11,0) = "TUTAR"
	Grid(12,0) = "ONAYKUTUSU"
	Grid(13,0) = "PARABIRIMI"
	Grid(14,0) = "STOK_NONSTOK"
	Grid(15,0) = "NONSTOK_AD"
	
	Grid(0,1) = 8
	Grid(1,1) = 30
	Grid(2,1) = 40
	Grid(3,1) = 35
	Grid(4,1) = 21
	Grid(5,1) = 17
	Grid(6,1) = 11
	Grid(7,1) = 30
	Grid(8,1) = 14
	Grid(9,1) = 12
	Grid(10,1) = 20
	Grid(11,1) = 20
	Grid(12,1) = 12
	Grid(13,1) = 3
	Grid(14,1) = 0
	Grid(15,1) = 0
	

	Grid(0,2) = "S. No"
	Grid(1,2) = "Malzeme Kodu"
	Grid(2,2) = "Malzeme Adý"
	Grid(3,2) = "Ne Ýçin Talep Ediliyor"
	Grid(4,2) = "Teslim Tarihi"
	Grid(5,2) = "Miktar"
	Grid(6,2) = "Birim"
	Grid(7,2) = "Teklif Alýnan Firma/lar"
	Grid(8,2) = "Ödeme Þekli"
	Grid(9,2) = "Vade"
	Grid(10,2) = "Birim Fiyat"
	Grid(11,2) = "Tutar"
	Grid(12,2) = "Onay"
	Grid(13,2) = "Para Birimi"
	Grid(14,2) = "NS"
	Grid(15,2) = "NS Açýklama"

	Grid(0,4) = "N"
	Grid(1,4) = "C"
	Grid(2,4) = "C"
	Grid(3,4) = "C"
	Grid(4,4) = "D"
	Grid(5,4) = "N"
	Grid(6,4) = "C"
	Grid(7,4) = "C"
	Grid(8,4) = "C"
	Grid(9,4) = "N"
	Grid(10,4) = "N"
	Grid(11,4) = "N"
	Grid(12,4) = "C"
	Grid(13,4) = "C"
	Grid(14,4) = "C"
	Grid(15,4) = "C"

	For i = 0 To UBound(Grid,1) 
		Degisken = Grid(i,0)
		If Grid(i,4)="N" And Left(degisken,1) <> "_" Then
			Degisken = Grid(i,0)
			ondalik = STOK47T.MaxSigDig(Degisken)	
			If Ondalik > 2 Then Ondalik = 2
			Grid(i,6) = Ondalik		
		End If
	Next	
End Sub
'----------------------------------------------------------------------------
Sub SayfaSayisiYazdir(pe1, Ilksayfa, SonSayfa)
	sayfaSayisi = SonSayfa - Ilksayfa + 1
	For i = Ilksayfa to SonSayfa
		sayfaNo = (i - Ilksayfa) + 1
		res = pe1.GotoPage(i)
		res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
		res = pe1.Write(ttt, kkk, satiryuksekligi, aaa, ": " & sayfaNo & " / " & sayfaSayisi)
	Next
End Sub
